                            if (hasTeamInfo) {
                                hasTeamInfo.style.display = 'none';
                            }
                            
                            // Hide date-time section for completed matches
                            const dt = card.querySelector('.match-date-time');
                            if (dt) dt.style.display = 'none';
                        }
                    }

                    if (statusContainer) {
                        statusContainer.innerHTML = isPost ? '<div class="fulltime-indicator"><span class="full-text">FULL TIME</span><span class="short-text">FT</span></div>' : '';
                    }

                    this.updateMatchActions(card, isPost ? 'completed' : 'pre');
                    // Stadium name only for pre-match; hide for post (result) and live (in-play)
                    const venueEl = card.querySelector('.match-venue');
                    if (venueEl) venueEl.style.display = isPost ? 'none' : '';
                }

                /**
                 * updateMatchActions(card, state)
                 */
                updateMatchActions(card, state) {
                    if (!card) return;
                    let matchActionsContainer = card.querySelector('.match-actions');
                    const dateTimeEl = card.querySelector('.match-date-time');

                    if (state === 'live') {
                        if (!matchActionsContainer) {
                            matchActionsContainer = document.createElement('div');
                            matchActionsContainer.className = 'match-actions';
                            const insertAfter = card.querySelector('.team-scores') || card.querySelector('.team-info');
                            if (insertAfter && insertAfter.parentNode) {
                                insertAfter.parentNode.insertBefore(matchActionsContainer, insertAfter.nextSibling);
                            } else {
                                card.appendChild(matchActionsContainer);
                            }
                        }
                        matchActionsContainer.innerHTML = `
                            <span class="dot"></span>
                            <span class="action-button follow-live">FOLLOW LIVE</span>
                        `;
                        if (dateTimeEl) dateTimeEl.style.display = 'none';
                    } else if (state === 'completed') {
                        if (!matchActionsContainer) {
                            matchActionsContainer = document.createElement('div');
                            matchActionsContainer.className = 'match-actions';
                            const insertAfter = card.querySelector('.team-scores') || card.querySelector('.team-info');
                            if (insertAfter && insertAfter.parentNode) {
                                insertAfter.parentNode.insertBefore(matchActionsContainer, insertAfter.nextSibling);
                            } else {
                                card.appendChild(matchActionsContainer);
                            }
                        }
                        matchActionsContainer.innerHTML = `
                            <span class="icon-recap"></span>
                            <span class="action-button recap">Recap</span>
                        `;
                        if (dateTimeEl) dateTimeEl.style.display = 'none';
                    } else {
                        if (matchActionsContainer) matchActionsContainer.remove();
                        if (dateTimeEl) dateTimeEl.style.display = '';
                    }
                }

                /**
                 * updateHomeHeroMatch(matchId, scoreData)
                 */
                updateHomeHeroMatch(matchId, scoreData) {
                    const homeHeroContainer = document.querySelector('.home-hero-container');
                    if (!homeHeroContainer) return;

                    const homeHeroMatch = homeHeroContainer.querySelector(`#match-${matchId}`);
                    if (!homeHeroMatch) {
                        if (scoreData.isLive) {
                            this.updateHomeHeroToShowLiveMatch(matchId, scoreData);
                        }
                        return;
                    }

                    const liveBtn = homeHeroMatch.querySelector('.live-btn');
                    if (liveBtn) {
                        if (scoreData.isLive) {
                            liveBtn.textContent = 'LIVE';
                            liveBtn.style.backgroundColor = '#ff4b5a';
                            liveBtn.style.color = '#ffffff';
                        } else if (scoreData.isCompleted) {
                            liveBtn.textContent = 'FULL TIME';
                            liveBtn.style.backgroundColor = '#00bcd4';
                            liveBtn.style.color = '#ffffff';
                        } else {
                            liveBtn.textContent = 'FIXTURE';
                            liveBtn.style.backgroundColor = '#666666';
                            liveBtn.style.color = '#ffffff';
                        }
                    }

                    const liveIndicators = homeHeroMatch.querySelectorAll('.live-indicator');
                    liveIndicators.forEach(indicator => {
                    if (scoreData.isLive) {
                            indicator.textContent = 'LIVE';
                            indicator.style.display = 'block';
                    } else if (scoreData.isCompleted) {
                            indicator.className = 'fulltime-indicator';
                            indicator.innerHTML = '<span class="full-text">FULL TIME</span><span class="short-text">FT</span>';
                            indicator.style.display = 'block';
                        } else {
                            indicator.style.display = 'none';
                        }
                    });
                }

                /**
                 * updateHomeHeroToShowLiveMatch(matchId, scoreData)
                 */
                updateHomeHeroToShowLiveMatch(matchId, scoreData) {
                    const homeHeroContainer = document.querySelector('.home-hero-container');
                    if (!homeHeroContainer) return;

                    const matchSection = homeHeroContainer.querySelector('.match-section');
                    if (matchSection) {
                        matchSection.classList.remove('post-match-bg');
                        matchSection.classList.add('live-match-bg');
                    }

                    const allLiveBtns = homeHeroContainer.querySelectorAll('.live-btn');
                    allLiveBtns.forEach(btn => {
                        btn.textContent = 'LIVE';
                        btn.style.backgroundColor = '#ff4b5a';
                        btn.style.color = '#ffffff';
                    });

                    const allLiveIndicators = homeHeroContainer.querySelectorAll('.live-indicator');
                    allLiveIndicators.forEach(ind => {
                        ind.textContent = 'LIVE';
                        ind.style.display = 'block';
                    });
                }

                /**
                 * updateHomeHeroState(matchId, scoreData)
                 */
                updateHomeHeroState(matchId, scoreData) {
                    const homeHeroContainer = document.querySelector('.home-hero-container');
                    if (!homeHeroContainer) return;
                    const matchSection = homeHeroContainer.querySelector('.match-section');
                    if (!matchSection) return;

                    const homeHeroMatch = homeHeroContainer.querySelector(`#match-${matchId}`);
                    const isCurrent = !!homeHeroMatch;
                    if (!isCurrent) return;

                    if (scoreData.isLive) {
                        matchSection.classList.remove('post-match-bg');
                        matchSection.classList.add('live-match-bg');
                    } else if (scoreData.isCompleted) {
                        matchSection.classList.remove('live-match-bg');
                        matchSection.classList.add('post-match-bg');
                    } else {
                        matchSection.classList.remove('live-match-bg', 'post-match-bg');
                    }

                    if (homeHeroContainer.classList.contains('splide')) {
                        window.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent('heroUpdated', { detail: { matchId, scoreData } }));
                    }
                }

                /**
                 * Switch hero template to live-match view
                 * Fetches live match content via AJAX and injects without page reload
                 */
                async switchHeroToLiveTemplate() {
                    // Prevent multiple simultaneous template switches
                    if (this.templateSwitchInProgress) {
                        // this.log('⚠️ Template switch already in progress, skipping');
                        return;
                    }
                    
                    // No time-based checks - allow immediate switches
                    this.templateSwitchInProgress = true;
                    
                    const homeHeroContainer = document.querySelector('.home-hero-container');
                    if (!homeHeroContainer) {
                        this.templateSwitchInProgress = false;
                        // this.log('❌ No home-hero-container found');
                        return;
                    }
                    
                    const matchSection = homeHeroContainer.querySelector('.match-section');
                    if (!matchSection) {
                        this.templateSwitchInProgress = false;
                        // this.log('❌ No match-section found');
                        return;
                    }
                    
                    try {
                        // Add timestamp to bust cache and ensure fresh Firebase data
                        const url = new URL(window.location.href);
                        url.searchParams.set('_refresh', Date.now());
                        
                        // If we have a specific match to show (from Firebase push), tell server to prioritize it
                        if (this.matchToShow) {
                            url.searchParams.set('_show_match', this.matchToShow);
                        }
                        
                        const response = await fetch(url.toString(), {
                            method: 'GET',
                            headers: {
                                'X-Requested-With': 'XMLHttpRequest',
                                'X-Hero-Refresh': 'true',
                                'Cache-Control': 'no-cache, no-store, must-revalidate'
                            }
                        });
                        
                        if (!response.ok) {
                            // this.log('❌ Fetch failed with status:', response.status);
                            // Clear the flag on error so it can retry
                            if (this.matchToShow) {
                                this.matchesThatTriggeredSwitch.delete(String(this.matchToShow));
                            }
                            return;
                        }

                        const html = await response.text();
                        const parser = new DOMParser();
                        const doc = parser.parseFromString(html, 'text/html');
                        
                        const newMatchSection = doc.querySelector('.home-hero-container .match-section');
                        if (!newMatchSection) {
                            // this.log('❌ No match-section found in fetched HTML');
                            // Clear the flag on error so it can retry
                            if (this.matchToShow) {
                                this.matchesThatTriggeredSwitch.delete(String(this.matchToShow));
                            }
                            return;
                        }
                        
                        const hasLiveMatch = newMatchSection.querySelector('.live-match-container');
                        if (!hasLiveMatch) {
                            // this.log('⚠️ New content does not contain live-match-container');
                            // Clear the flag if content doesn't have live match (might be timing issue)
                            if (this.matchToShow) {
                                this.matchesThatTriggeredSwitch.delete(String(this.matchToShow));
                            }
                            return;
                        }
                        
                        // Destroy existing Splide instance before updating DOM
                        if (homeHeroContainer.classList.contains('splide')) {
                            const existingSplide = homeHeroContainer.splide;
                            if (existingSplide && typeof existingSplide.destroy === 'function') {
                                try {
                                    // Remove is-initialized class before destroying to reset CSS
                                    homeHeroContainer.classList.remove('is-initialized');
                                    existingSplide.destroy();
                                } catch (e) {
                                    // this.log('⚠️ Error destroying Splide', e);
                                }
                                homeHeroContainer.splide = null;
                            }
                            homeHeroContainer.classList.remove('splide');
                            homeHeroContainer.classList.remove('is-initialized');
                        }
                        
                        // Update the match section content immediately - no delays
                        matchSection.innerHTML = newMatchSection.innerHTML;
                        matchSection.className = newMatchSection.className;
                        
                        this.currentHeroState = 'in-play';
                        
                        // Update current hero match ID
                        this.detectCurrentHeroMatch();
                        
                        // Clear matchToShow since we've switched
                        if (this.matchToShow) {
                            const switchedMatchId = String(this.matchToShow);
                            // Clear immediately - no delays needed
                            // Allow immediate re-triggering for new Firebase pushes
                            this.matchesThatTriggeredSwitch.delete(switchedMatchId);
                            this.matchToShow = null;
                        }
                        
                        // Check if we need to initialize Splide based on actual live match count
                        const actuallyLiveMatches = this.getActuallyLiveMatches();
                        const liveMatchCount = actuallyLiveMatches.length;
                        
                        // Count actual live match containers in the new DOM
                        const newLiveMatchContainers = newMatchSection.querySelectorAll('.live-match-container');
                        const newLiveMatchCount = newLiveMatchContainers.length;
                        
                        // Only initialize Splide if we have 2+ live matches in both cache and DOM
                        if (liveMatchCount >= 2 && newLiveMatchCount >= 2) {
                            const newHeroContainer = doc.querySelector('.home-hero-container');
                            const shouldUseSplide = newHeroContainer && newHeroContainer.classList.contains('splide');
                            
                            if (shouldUseSplide) {
                                homeHeroContainer.classList.add('splide');
                                
                                // Ensure Splide structure exists before initializing
                                const track = homeHeroContainer.querySelector('.splide__track');
                                const list = homeHeroContainer.querySelector('.splide__list');
                                const slides = homeHeroContainer.querySelectorAll('.splide__slide');
                                
                                if (track && list && slides.length >= 2) {
                                    // Initialize Splide immediately - no delays, no RAFs
                                    // Only initialize if not already initializing and structure is still valid
                                    if (!this.splideInitializing) {
                                        const stillHasStructure = homeHeroContainer.querySelector('.splide__track') && 
                                                                  homeHeroContainer.querySelectorAll('.splide__slide').length >= 2;
                                        if (stillHasStructure) {
                                            this.initializeSplideForLiveMatches();
                                        } else {
                                            this.splideInitializing = false;
                                        }
                                    }
                                } else {
                                    // Structure not ready - clear flag
                                    this.splideInitializing = false;
                                }
                            } else {
                                // Server didn't return Splide structure, ensure it's removed
                                if (homeHeroContainer.classList.contains('splide')) {
                                    homeHeroContainer.classList.remove('splide');
                                }
                                this.splideInitializing = false;
                            }
                        } else {
                            // Less than 2 live matches - ensure Splide is removed and DOM shows only live match
                            if (homeHeroContainer.classList.contains('splide')) {
                                homeHeroContainer.classList.remove('splide');
                                if (homeHeroContainer.splide && typeof homeHeroContainer.splide.destroy === 'function') {
                                    try {
                                        homeHeroContainer.splide.destroy();
                                    } catch (e) {}
                                    homeHeroContainer.splide = null;
                                }
                                if (homeHeroContainer.splideInstance && typeof homeHeroContainer.splideInstance.destroy === 'function') {
                                    try {
                                        homeHeroContainer.splideInstance.destroy();
                                    } catch (e) {}
                                    homeHeroContainer.splideInstance = null;
                                }
                            }
                            this.splideInitializing = false;
                        }
                            
                            window.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent('heroTemplateSwitched', {
                                detail: { state: 'in-play' }
                            }));
                        
                        // Re-initialize Blaze Stories widgets after template switch
                        this.reinitializeBlazeStories();
                        
                        // this.log('✅ Template switch completed successfully');
                        
                    } catch (error) {
                        // this.log('❌ Error during template switch', error);
                    } finally {
                        // Always clear the flag, even on error
                        this.templateSwitchInProgress = false;
                    }
                }

                /**
                 * Switch hero template back to post-match/fixture view
                 * Fetches updated content via AJAX
                 */
                async switchHeroToPostMatchTemplate() {
                    // this.log('🔄 Starting switch to post-match template...');
                    
                    // Prevent multiple simultaneous template switches
                    if (this.templateSwitchInProgress) {
                        // this.log('⚠️ Template switch already in progress, skipping');
                        return;
                    }
                    
                    // No time-based checks - allow immediate switches
                    this.templateSwitchInProgress = true;

                    const homeHeroContainer = document.querySelector('.home-hero-container');
                    if (!homeHeroContainer) {
                        this.templateSwitchInProgress = false;
                        // this.log('❌ No home-hero-container found');
                        return;
                    }
                    
                    const matchSection = homeHeroContainer.querySelector('.match-section');
                    if (!matchSection) {
                        this.templateSwitchInProgress = false;
                        // this.log('❌ No match-section found');
                        return;
                    }
                    
                    // No delay - proceed immediately
                    // Removed setTimeout to prevent delays
                    
                    try {
                        // this.log('📡 Fetching updated page content...');
                        
                        // Add timestamp to bust cache
                        const url = new URL(window.location.href);
                        url.searchParams.set('_refresh', Date.now());
                        
                        const response = await fetch(url.toString(), {
                            method: 'GET',
                            headers: {
                                'X-Requested-With': 'XMLHttpRequest',
                                'X-Hero-Refresh': 'true',
                                'Cache-Control': 'no-cache, no-store, must-revalidate'
                            }
                        });
                        
                        // this.log('📥 Fetch response received', { status: response.status, ok: response.ok });
                        
                        if (!response.ok) {
                            // this.log('❌ Fetch failed');
                            return;
                        }

                        const html = await response.text();
                        // this.log('📄 HTML received, length:', html.length);
                        
                        const parser = new DOMParser();
                        const doc = parser.parseFromString(html, 'text/html');
                        
                        const newMatchSection = doc.querySelector('.home-hero-container .match-section');
                        if (!newMatchSection) {
                            // this.log('❌ No match-section found in fetched HTML');
                            return;
                        }
                        
                        // this.log('✏️ Updating DOM with new content...');
                            matchSection.innerHTML = newMatchSection.innerHTML;
                            matchSection.className = newMatchSection.className;
                        
                        const hasLiveMatch = newMatchSection.querySelector('.live-match-container');
                        this.currentHeroState = hasLiveMatch ? 'in-play' : 'fixture';
                        
                        // Update current hero match ID
                        this.currentHeroMatchId = null; // Clear it, will be detected on next check
                        this.detectCurrentHeroMatch();
                        
                        // this.log('✅ Match section updated successfully, new state:', this.currentHeroState);
                        
                        if (homeHeroContainer.classList.contains('splide')) {
                            homeHeroContainer.classList.remove('splide');
                            const splideInstance = homeHeroContainer.splide;
                            if (splideInstance && typeof splideInstance.destroy === 'function') {
                                splideInstance.destroy();
                            }
                        }
                            
                            window.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent('heroTemplateSwitched', {
                            detail: { state: this.currentHeroState } 
                        }));
                        
                        // Re-initialize Blaze Stories widgets after template switch
                        this.reinitializeBlazeStories();
                        
                        // this.log('🎉 Template switch to post-match completed');
                        
                    } catch (error) {
                        // this.log('❌ Error during template switch', error);
                    } finally {
                        // Always clear the flag, even on error
                        this.templateSwitchInProgress = false;
                    }
                }

                /**
                 * Clean up completed matches from cache
                 */
                cleanupCompletedMatches() {
                    const toRemove = [];
                    
                    this.liveMatchesCache.forEach((matchData, matchId) => {
                        // Remove if completed or not live
                        if (matchData.isCompleted || !matchData.isLive) {
                            toRemove.push(matchId);
                        }
                    });
                    
                    toRemove.forEach(matchId => {
                        this.liveMatchesCache.delete(matchId);
                        if (this.currentHeroMatchId === matchId) {
                            this.currentHeroMatchId = null;
                        }
                        // this.log(`🧹 Cleaned up match ${matchId} from cache (completed or not live)`);
                    });
                    
                    // if (toRemove.length > 0) {
                    //     this.log(`🧹 Cleaned up ${toRemove.length} completed/stale match(es) from cache`);
                    // }
                }

                /**
                 * Reconcile fixture cards that are still marked live in DOM but are no longer live in cache.
                 * This handles missed final status events during concurrent live matches.
                 */
                reconcileLiveFixtureCards() {
                    const liveCards = document.querySelectorAll('.fixtures-slider .fixture-card.fixture-live[data-match-id]');
                    if (!liveCards || !liveCards.length) {
                        return;
                    }

                    liveCards.forEach((card) => {
                        const matchId = String(card.getAttribute('data-match-id') || '').trim();
                        if (!matchId) return;

                        // Only reconcile cards that have received realtime updates in this session.
                        const seenAtRaw = card.getAttribute('data-firebase-seen-at');
                        const seenAt = seenAtRaw ? Number(seenAtRaw) : NaN;
                        if (!Number.isFinite(seenAt)) return;
                        if (Date.now() - seenAt < 3000) return;
                        // If cache still marks it live, leave it.
                        if (this.liveMatchesCache.has(matchId)) return;

                        const homeEl = card.querySelector('.score[data-team="home"]');
                        const awayEl = card.querySelector('.score[data-team="away"]');
                        const home = homeEl ? parseInt((homeEl.textContent || '').trim(), 10) : null;
                        const away = awayEl ? parseInt((awayEl.textContent || '').trim(), 10) : null;

                        this.updateLiveStatus(card, false, {
                            home: Number.isFinite(home) ? home : null,
                            away: Number.isFinite(away) ? away : null,
                            isLive: false,
                            isCompleted: true,
                            hasScoreData: !!(homeEl || awayEl),
                            period: 'post',
                            matchStatus: 'completed'
                        });
                    });
                }
                
                /**
                 * Get actually live matches by verifying their status from cache
                 * Only returns matches that are truly live (not completed)
                 */
                getActuallyLiveMatches() {
                    const actuallyLive = [];
                    
                    this.liveMatchesCache.forEach((matchData, matchId) => {
                        // Verify the match is actually live (not completed)
                        // matchData can be either direct scoreData or {scoreData, lastUpdated}
                        const isLive = matchData.isLive === true;
                        const isCompleted = matchData.isCompleted === true;
                        
                        if (isLive && !isCompleted) {
                            actuallyLive.push({
                                matchId: matchId,
                                data: matchData
                            });
                        }
                    });
                    
                    return actuallyLive;
                }

                /**
                 * Immediate template switch - no debouncing, no delays
                 * Removed debouncing to prevent flicker and enable instant switches
                 */
                debouncedTemplateSwitch() {
                    // Call checkHeroTemplateSwitch immediately - no debouncing
                    this.checkHeroTemplateSwitch();
                }

                /**
                 * Check if hero template needs to switch from fixture/results to live-match
                 * This verifies actual live status, not just cache presence
                 */
                checkHeroTemplateSwitch() {
                    // Prevent recursive calls - if already checking, skip
                    if (this.checkHeroTemplateSwitchInProgress) {
                        return;
                    }
                    
                    // Don't switch if one is already in progress
                    if (this.templateSwitchInProgress) {
                        return;
                    }
                    
                    const homeHeroContainer = document.querySelector('.home-hero-container');
                    
                    if (!homeHeroContainer) {
                        // this.log('⚠️ No home-hero-container found, skipping template switch check');
                        return;
                    }
                    
                    const matchSection = homeHeroContainer.querySelector('.match-section');
                    if (!matchSection) {
                        // this.log('⚠️ No match-section found, skipping template switch check');
                        return;
                    }
                    
                    // Set flag to prevent recursive calls
                    this.checkHeroTemplateSwitchInProgress = true;
                    
                    try {
                        // Get actually live matches (verify they're not completed)
                        const actuallyLiveMatches = this.getActuallyLiveMatches();
                        const actuallyLiveCount = actuallyLiveMatches.length;
                        const actuallyLiveMatchIds = actuallyLiveMatches.map(m => m.matchId);
                        
                        const hasLiveMatchContainer = !!document.querySelector('.live-match-container');
                        
                        // Detect which match is currently displayed in home-hero
                        const currentDisplayedMatch = this.detectCurrentHeroMatch();
                        const currentMatchIsActuallyLive = currentDisplayedMatch && actuallyLiveMatchIds.includes(String(currentDisplayedMatch));
                        
                        // this.log('🔍 Checking hero template switch', {
                        //     cacheSize: this.liveMatchesCache.size,
                        //     actuallyLiveCount: actuallyLiveCount,
                        //     currentHeroState: this.currentHeroState,
                        //     hasLiveMatchContainer: hasLiveMatchContainer,
                        //     currentDisplayedMatch: currentDisplayedMatch,
                        //     currentMatchIsActuallyLive: currentMatchIsActuallyLive,
                        //     actuallyLiveMatches: actuallyLiveMatchIds
                        // });

                        // SCENARIO 2 first: leave live hero when nothing is actually live.
                        // Must NOT be blocked by splideInitializing (that flag only applies to live/Splide setup).
                        if (actuallyLiveCount === 0 && hasLiveMatchContainer) {
                            this.switchHeroToPostMatchTemplate();
                            return;
                        }

                        if (this.splideInitializing) {
                            return;
                        }
                    
                        // SCENARIO 1: Switch TO live template if we have actually live matches but not showing live view
                        if (actuallyLiveCount > 0 && !hasLiveMatchContainer) {
                            this.switchHeroToLiveTemplate();
                        }
                        // SCENARIO 2 handled above
                        // SCENARIO 3: Currently displayed match is no longer actually live, but other matches are live
                        else if (hasLiveMatchContainer && currentDisplayedMatch && !currentMatchIsActuallyLive && actuallyLiveCount > 0) {
                            this.switchHeroToLiveTemplate();
                        }
                        // SCENARIO 4: Current match not in actually live matches, refresh
                        else if (hasLiveMatchContainer && actuallyLiveCount > 0 && currentDisplayedMatch && !actuallyLiveMatchIds.includes(String(currentDisplayedMatch))) {
                            this.switchHeroToLiveTemplate();
                        }
                        // SCENARIO 5: Multiple actually live matches but Splide not active
                        else if (hasLiveMatchContainer && actuallyLiveCount >= 2 && !homeHeroContainer.classList.contains('splide')) {
                            this.switchHeroToLiveTemplate();
                        }
                        // SCENARIO 6: Only 1 actually live match but Splide is active, remove Splide and refresh
                        else if (hasLiveMatchContainer && actuallyLiveCount === 1 && homeHeroContainer.classList.contains('splide')) {
                            // Always refresh template when going from 2+ to 1 match to ensure completed match is removed
                            // This ensures the DOM is updated to show only the live match
                            this.switchHeroToLiveTemplate();
                        }
                        // SCENARIO 7: Check if a more recently updated match should be shown (Firebase push priority)
                        else if (hasLiveMatchContainer && actuallyLiveCount > 0 && currentDisplayedMatch) {
                            // Find the most recently updated match
                            let mostRecentMatchId = null;
                            let mostRecentTime = 0;
                            
                            actuallyLiveMatchIds.forEach(matchId => {
                                const matchData = this.liveMatchesCache.get(matchId);
                                if (matchData) {
                                    const lastUpdated = matchData.lastUpdated || 0;
                                    
                                    // Prioritize by lastUpdated (most recently updated match)
                                    if (lastUpdated > mostRecentTime) {
                                        mostRecentTime = lastUpdated;
                                        mostRecentMatchId = matchId;
                                    }
                                }
                            });
                            
                            // If we found a most recent match and it's different from current, switch immediately
                            if (mostRecentMatchId && mostRecentMatchId !== String(currentDisplayedMatch)) {
                                // Switch immediately - no time-based checks, no delays
                                this.switchHeroToLiveTemplate();
                            }
                        }
                    } finally {
                        // Always clear the flag
                        this.checkHeroTemplateSwitchInProgress = false;
                    }
                }
                
                /**
                 * Detect which match ID is currently displayed in home-hero
                 */
                detectCurrentHeroMatch() {
                    const homeHeroContainer = document.querySelector('.home-hero-container');
                    if (!homeHeroContainer) return null;
                    
                    // If Splide is active, get the active slide
                    let activeContainer = homeHeroContainer;
                    if (homeHeroContainer.classList.contains('splide')) {
                        const activeSlide = homeHeroContainer.querySelector('.splide__slide.is-active, .splide__slide.is-visible');
                        if (activeSlide) {
                            activeContainer = activeSlide;
                        }
                    }
                    
                    // Try to find match ID from id attribute (format: match-{id})
                    const matchElement = activeContainer.querySelector('[id^="match-"]');
                    if (matchElement) {
                        const matchId = matchElement.id.replace('match-', '');
                        if (matchId && !isNaN(matchId)) {
                            this.currentHeroMatchId = matchId;
                            // this.log(`📍 Detected current hero match ID: ${matchId} (from id attribute)`);
                            return matchId;
                        }
                    }
                    
                    // Try to find from data-match-id attribute
                    const dataMatchElement = activeContainer.querySelector('[data-match-id]');
                    if (dataMatchElement) {
                        const matchId = dataMatchElement.getAttribute('data-match-id');
                        if (matchId && !isNaN(matchId)) {
                            this.currentHeroMatchId = matchId;
                            // this.log(`📍 Detected current hero match ID: ${matchId} (from data-match-id)`);
                            return matchId;
                        }
                    }
                    
                    // Try to find from live match container (fallback)
                    const liveMatchContainer = activeContainer.querySelector('.live-match-container');
                    if (liveMatchContainer) {
                        // Check if it has an id
                        if (liveMatchContainer.id && liveMatchContainer.id.startsWith('match-')) {
                            const matchId = liveMatchContainer.id.replace('match-', '');
                            if (matchId && !isNaN(matchId)) {
                                this.currentHeroMatchId = matchId;
                                // this.log(`📍 Detected current hero match ID: ${matchId} (from live-match-container id)`);
                                return matchId;
                            }
                        }
                        // Check data attribute
                        const matchIdAttr = liveMatchContainer.getAttribute('data-match-id');
                        if (matchIdAttr && !isNaN(matchIdAttr)) {
                            this.currentHeroMatchId = matchIdAttr;
                            // this.log(`📍 Detected current hero match ID: ${matchIdAttr} (from live-match-container data)`);
                            return matchIdAttr;
                        }
                    }
                    
                    // Return cached value if no new detection
                    // if (this.currentHeroMatchId) {
                    //     this.log(`📍 Using cached hero match ID: ${this.currentHeroMatchId}`);
                    // }
                    return this.currentHeroMatchId;
                }

                /**
                 * Update current match ID from active Splide slide
                 */
                updateCurrentMatchFromSplide(container) {
                    if (!container) return;
                    
                    const activeSlide = container.querySelector('.splide__slide.is-active, .splide__slide.is-visible');
                    if (activeSlide) {
                        const matchContainer = activeSlide.querySelector('.live-match-container');
                        if (matchContainer) {
                            // Try ID first
                            if (matchContainer.id && matchContainer.id.startsWith('match-')) {
                                const matchId = matchContainer.id.replace('match-', '');
                                if (matchId && !isNaN(matchId)) {
                                    this.currentHeroMatchId = matchId;
                                    return;
                                }
                            }
                            // Try data attribute
                            const matchIdAttr = matchContainer.getAttribute('data-match-id');
                            if (matchIdAttr && !isNaN(matchIdAttr)) {
                                this.currentHeroMatchId = matchIdAttr;
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }

                /**
                 * After bfcache restore, Splide's list transform and slide metrics are often wrong — refresh or re-init.
                 */
                refreshHomeHeroSplideAfterBfCache() {
                    const homeHeroContainer = document.querySelector('.home-hero-container');
                    if (!homeHeroContainer || !homeHeroContainer.classList.contains('splide')) {
                        return;
                    }

                    const apply = () => {
                        if (typeof Splide === 'undefined') {
                            if (typeof window.splideWhenReady === 'function') {
                                window.splideWhenReady(() => this.refreshHomeHeroSplideAfterBfCache());
                            }
                            return;
                        }

                        const splide = homeHeroContainer.splide;
                        if (splide && typeof splide.refresh === 'function') {
                            try {
                                if (splide.state && splide.state.is(Splide.STATES.DESTROYED)) {
                                    this.splideInitializing = false;
                                    this.initializeSplideForLiveMatches();
                                    return;
                                }
                                homeHeroContainer.offsetHeight;
                                splide.refresh();
                                requestAnimationFrame(() => {
                                    splide.refresh();
                                });
                            } catch (err) {
                                this.splideInitializing = false;
                                this.initializeSplideForLiveMatches();
                            }
                            return;
                        }

                        this.splideInitializing = false;
                        this.initializeSplideForLiveMatches();
                    };

                    apply();
                }

                /**
                 * Initialize or reinitialize Splide for multiple live matches
                 */
                initializeSplideForLiveMatches() {
                    // this.log('🎠 initializeSplideForLiveMatches called');
                    
                    const homeHeroContainer = document.querySelector('.home-hero-container');
                    if (!homeHeroContainer) {
                        // this.log('❌ No home-hero-container found');
                        return;
                    }
                    
                    // Prevent duplicate initialization - check if Splide is already initialized
                    if (homeHeroContainer.splide && typeof homeHeroContainer.splide.mount === 'function') {
                        // this.log('⚠️ Splide already initialized, skipping');
                        return;
                    }
                    
                    // Prevent initialization if already in progress
                    if (this.splideInitializing) {
                        // this.log('⚠️ Splide initialization already in progress, skipping');
                        return;
                    }
                    
                    // Set flag to prevent concurrent initialization
                    this.splideInitializing = true;
                    
                    try {
                        // Get actually live matches (not just cache size)
                        const actuallyLiveMatches = this.getActuallyLiveMatches();
                        const actuallyLiveCount = actuallyLiveMatches.length;
                        // this.log('🎠 Actually live match count:', actuallyLiveCount);
                        
                        // Only initialize Splide if we have 2+ actually live matches
                        if (actuallyLiveCount >= 2) {
                            // this.log('🎠 2+ live matches, initializing Splide');
                            
                            // Check if Splide library is loaded BEFORE adding the splide class
                            // This prevents the delay where only one slide shows
                            if (typeof Splide === 'undefined') {
                                // Use global loader if available, else fallback to fetchInject
                                if (typeof window.splideWhenReady === 'function') {
                                    window.splideWhenReady(() => {
                                        if (!homeHeroContainer.classList.contains('splide')) {
                                            homeHeroContainer.classList.add('splide');
                                            homeHeroContainer.classList.add('is-initialized');
                                        }
                                        this.initSplideInstance(homeHeroContainer);
                                    });
                                } else if (window.fetchInject && window.SPLIDE_CDN_URL) {
                                    window.fetchInject([window.SPLIDE_CDN_URL])
                                        .then(() => {
                                            if (!homeHeroContainer.classList.contains('splide')) {
                                                homeHeroContainer.classList.add('splide');
                                                homeHeroContainer.classList.add('is-initialized');
                                            }
                                            this.initSplideInstance(homeHeroContainer);
                                        })
                                        .catch(() => { this.splideInitializing = false; });
                                } else {
                                    this.splideInitializing = false;
                                }
                            } else {
                                // Splide already loaded - add class and initialize immediately
                                if (!homeHeroContainer.classList.contains('splide')) {
                                    homeHeroContainer.classList.add('splide');
                                    // Add is-initialized immediately to prevent the "only first slide" flash
                                    // The mounted event will properly configure everything
                                    homeHeroContainer.classList.add('is-initialized');
                                }
                                // Initialize synchronously (no RAF delay)
                                this.initSplideInstance(homeHeroContainer);
                            }
                        } else if (actuallyLiveCount < 2) {
                            // Remove Splide for single or no live matches
                            if (homeHeroContainer.classList.contains('splide')) {
                                homeHeroContainer.classList.remove('splide');
                                const splideInstance = homeHeroContainer.splide;
                                if (splideInstance && typeof splideInstance.destroy === 'function') {
                                    try {
                                        splideInstance.destroy();
                                    } catch (e) {
                                        // this.log('⚠️ Error destroying Splide', e);
                                    }
                                    homeHeroContainer.splide = null;
                                }
                            }
                            this.splideInitializing = false;
                        }
                    } catch (error) {
                        // this.log('❌ Error in initializeSplideForLiveMatches', error);
                        this.splideInitializing = false;
                    }
                }

                /**
                 * Initialize Splide instance on container
                 */
                initSplideInstance(container) {
                    if (!container || typeof Splide === 'undefined') {
                        // this.log('❌ Cannot init Splide - container or Splide undefined');
                        this.splideInitializing = false;
                        return;
                    }
                    
                    // Destroy existing instance if present
                    if (container.splide && typeof container.splide.destroy === 'function') {
                        try {
                            // Remove is-initialized class before destroying to reset CSS
                            container.classList.remove('is-initialized');
                            container.splide.destroy();
                        } catch (e) {
                            // Ignore destroy errors
                        }
                        container.splide = null;
                    } else {
                        // Ensure is-initialized class is removed if no Splide instance exists
                        container.classList.remove('is-initialized');
                    }
                        
                    // Verify we have slides before initializing - with multiple checks
                    const track = container.querySelector('.splide__track');
                    const list = container.querySelector('.splide__list');
                    const slides = container.querySelectorAll('.splide__slide');
                    
                    // Robust validation - ensure all required elements exist
                    if (!track || !list || slides.length === 0) {
                        // this.log('⚠️ No Splide track, list, or slides found, cannot initialize');
                        this.splideInitializing = false;
                        return;
                    }
                    
                    // Additional validation - ensure slides have content
                    const slidesWithContent = Array.from(slides).filter(slide => {
                        const matchContainer = slide.querySelector('.live-match-container');
                        return matchContainer && matchContainer.children.length > 0;
                    });
                    
                    if (slidesWithContent.length < 2) {
                        // this.log(`⚠️ Only ${slidesWithContent.length} slides with content, cannot initialize Splide (need 2+)`);
                        this.splideInitializing = false;
                        return;
                    }
                    
                    // Get actually live matches
                    const actuallyLiveMatches = this.getActuallyLiveMatches();
                    const actuallyLiveMatchIds = actuallyLiveMatches.map(m => m.matchId);
                    
                    // Filter slides to only include actually live matches (prevent duplicates)
                    const validSlides = Array.from(slides).filter(slide => {
                        const matchContainer = slide.querySelector('.live-match-container');
                        if (!matchContainer) return false;
                        
                        // Ensure slide has proper structure
                        if (!matchContainer.children || matchContainer.children.length === 0) {
                            return false;
                        }
                        
                        let matchId = null;
                        if (matchContainer.id && matchContainer.id.startsWith('match-')) {
                            matchId = matchContainer.id.replace('match-', '');
                        } else {
                            matchId = matchContainer.getAttribute('data-match-id');
                        }
                        
                        // Only include if match is actually live
                        return matchId && actuallyLiveMatchIds.includes(String(matchId));
                    });
                    
                    if (validSlides.length < 2) {
                        // this.log(`⚠️ Only ${validSlides.length} valid live match slides, cannot initialize Splide (need 2+)`);
                        this.splideInitializing = false;
                        return;
                    }
                    
                    // Ensure container is visible and has proper dimensions before initializing
                    const containerRect = container.getBoundingClientRect();
                    if (containerRect.width === 0 || containerRect.height === 0) {
                        // Container not ready - try immediate reflow
                        container.offsetHeight; // Force reflow
                        const retryRect = container.getBoundingClientRect();
                        if (retryRect.width === 0 || retryRect.height === 0) {
                            // Container still not ready - this shouldn't happen in normal cases
                            // Continue anyway and let Splide handle it
                            // this.log('⚠️ Container dimensions are 0, but continuing with Splide init');
                        }
                    }
                    
                    try {
                        // Get actually live match IDs to determine which slide should be active
                        let initialIndex = 0;
                        
                        // Try to find the slide with the current match or most recent live match
                        if (actuallyLiveMatchIds.length > 0) {
                            // First, try to find current match
                            if (this.currentHeroMatchId) {
                                const currentMatchIndex = actuallyLiveMatchIds.indexOf(String(this.currentHeroMatchId));
                                if (currentMatchIndex >= 0) {
                                    // Find the slide index for this match
                                    validSlides.forEach((slide, index) => {
                                        const matchContainer = slide.querySelector('.live-match-container');
                                        if (matchContainer) {
                                            let matchId = null;
                                            if (matchContainer.id && matchContainer.id.startsWith('match-')) {
                                                matchId = matchContainer.id.replace('match-', '');
                                            } else {
                                                matchId = matchContainer.getAttribute('data-match-id');
                                            }
                                            if (matchId === String(this.currentHeroMatchId)) {
                                                initialIndex = index;
                                                // this.log(`🎠 Found current match at slide index ${index}`);
                                            }
                                        }
                                    });
                                }
                            }
                            
                            // If not found, use most recently updated match
                            if (initialIndex === 0 && actuallyLiveMatches.length > 0) {
                                let mostRecentMatchId = null;
                                let mostRecentTime = 0;
                                
                                actuallyLiveMatches.forEach(({matchId, data}) => {
                                    if (data.lastUpdated && data.lastUpdated > mostRecentTime) {
                                        mostRecentTime = data.lastUpdated;
                                        mostRecentMatchId = matchId;
                                    }
                                });
                                
                                if (mostRecentMatchId) {
                                    validSlides.forEach((slide, index) => {
                                        const matchContainer = slide.querySelector('.live-match-container');
                                        if (matchContainer) {
                                            let matchId = null;
                                            if (matchContainer.id && matchContainer.id.startsWith('match-')) {
                                                matchId = matchContainer.id.replace('match-', '');
                                            } else {
                                                matchId = matchContainer.getAttribute('data-match-id');
                                            }
                                            if (matchId === String(mostRecentMatchId)) {
                                                initialIndex = index;
                                            }
                                        }
                                    });
                                }
                            }
                        }
                        
                        const config = {
                            type: 'loop',
                            perPage: 1,
                            perMove: 1,
                            arrows: false,
                            pagination: true,
                            autoplay: {
                                enabled: true,
                                interval: 3000, // Auto-advance every 3 seconds (standard interval)
                                pauseOnHover: false,
                                pauseOnFocus: false
                            },
                            drag: true,
                            draggable: true,
                            speed: 0, // No animation - instant transition
                            gap: '0px',
                            autoHeight: false,
                            start: initialIndex, // Start at the correct slide
                            focus: 'center', // Center the active slide
                            trimSpace: false, // Don't remove empty slides
                            updateOnMove: false, // Prevent automatic updates on move
                            lazyLoad: false, // Disable lazy loading
                            easing: 'linear', // Linear easing for instant feel
                            rewind: false, // Disable rewind animation
                            rewindSpeed: 0, // No rewind animation
                            waitForTransition: false, // Don't wait for transitions
                            omitEnd: false // Keep all slides for loop
                        };
                        
                        // Add CSS to disable all transitions and animations for instant switching
                        const styleId = 'firebase-splide-no-anim';
                        if (!document.getElementById(styleId)) {
                            const style = document.createElement('style');
                            style.id = styleId;
                            style.textContent = `
                                .home-hero-container.splide .splide__track,
                                .home-hero-container.splide .splide__list,
                                .home-hero-container.splide .splide__slide,
                                .home-hero-container.splide .splide__slide * {
                                    transition: none !important;
                                    animation: none !important;
                                }
                                .home-hero-container.splide .splide__track {
                                    will-change: auto !important;
                                }
                                .home-hero-container.splide .splide__list {
                                    transition: transform 0s !important;
                                }
                                .home-hero-container.splide .splide__slide {
                                    transition: transform 0s, opacity 0s !important;
                                    backface-visibility: hidden;
                                    -webkit-backface-visibility: hidden;
                                    transform: translateZ(0);
                                    -webkit-transform: translateZ(0);
                                }
                            `;
                            document.head.appendChild(style);
                        }
                        
                        // Ensure container is ready before creating Splide instance
                        // Force layout recalculation to prevent layout breaks
                        container.offsetHeight; // Trigger reflow
                        
                        const splideInstance = new Splide(container, config);
                        
                        // Mount Splide with error handling
                        try {
                            splideInstance.mount();
                            
                            // Store instance reference (both for Firebase and live-match-splide)
                            container.splide = splideInstance;
                            container.splideInstance = splideInstance;
                            
                            // Force layout stabilization immediately - no RAF delays
                            // Ensure all slides maintain proper dimensions
                            validSlides.forEach(slide => {
                                slide.style.minWidth = '100%';
                                slide.style.width = '100%';
                                slide.style.flexShrink = '0';
                            });
                            
                            // Ensure track maintains width
                            if (track) {
                                track.style.width = '100%';
                            }
                            if (list) {
                                list.style.width = 'auto';
                                list.style.display = 'flex';
                            }
                        } catch (mountError) {
                            // this.log('❌ Error mounting Splide', mountError);
                            this.splideInitializing = false;
                            return;
                        }
                        
                        // Update current match ID after Splide is mounted
                        splideInstance.on('mounted', () => {
                            // Ensure container is marked as initialized (may already be set)
                            if (!container.classList.contains('is-initialized')) {
                                container.classList.add('is-initialized');
                            }
                            
                            // Reset slide positions - let Splide handle positioning
                            const slides = container.querySelectorAll('.splide__slide');
                            slides.forEach(slide => {
                                slide.style.position = 'relative';
                                slide.style.opacity = '1';
                                slide.style.visibility = 'visible';
                                slide.style.pointerEvents = 'auto';
                                slide.style.left = 'auto';
                                slide.style.top = 'auto';
                                slide.style.transition = 'none';
                                slide.style.minWidth = '100%';
                                slide.style.width = '100%';
                                slide.style.flexShrink = '0';
                            });
                            
                            // Configure list
                            const list = container.querySelector('.splide__list');
                            if (list) {
                                list.style.transition = 'none';
                                list.style.width = 'auto';
                                list.style.display = 'flex';
                                list.style.position = 'relative';
                            }
                            
                            // Ensure track maintains proper dimensions
                            const track = container.querySelector('.splide__track');
                            if (track) {
                                track.style.width = '100%';
                                track.style.overflow = 'hidden';
                                track.style.position = 'relative';
                            }
                            
                            this.updateCurrentMatchFromSplide(container);
                            // Clear initialization flag
                            this.splideInitializing = false;
                            // this.log('✅ Splide mounted successfully');
                        });
                        
                        // Listen for Splide slide changes (without refresh)
                        splideInstance.on('moved', (newIndex) => {
                            // Update match ID immediately without any delays
                            this.updateCurrentMatchFromSplide(container);
                            
                            // Force instant positioning to prevent flicker - no RAF delays
                            const list = container.querySelector('.splide__list');
                            if (list) {
                                list.style.transition = 'none';
                            }
                            const slides = container.querySelectorAll('.splide__slide');
                            slides.forEach(slide => {
                                slide.style.transition = 'none';
                            });
                        });
                        
                        // Prevent flicker on move start
                        splideInstance.on('move', () => {
                            const list = container.querySelector('.splide__list');
                            if (list) {
                                list.style.transition = 'none';
                            }
                        });
                        
                        // Refresh on resize immediately - no debouncing
                        const resizeHandler = () => {
                            if (splideInstance && !splideInstance.state.is(Splide.STATES.DESTROYED)) {
                                splideInstance.refresh();
                            }
                        };
                        window.addEventListener('resize', resizeHandler);
                        
                        // Dispatch event
                        window.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent('heroUpdated'));
                    } catch (error) {
                        // this.log('❌ Error initializing Splide', error);
                        // Clear initialization flag on error
                        this.splideInitializing = false;
                    }
                }

                /**
                 * Update background image based on match state
                 * Fetches fixture image or fallback as per home-hero logic
                 */
                updateHeroBackgroundImage(matchId) {
                    const matchSection = document.querySelector('.home-hero-container .match-section');
                    if (!matchSection) return;
                    
                    // For live matches, prefer fixture image or fallback
                    // This would need to be coordinated with server-side logic
                    // For now, keep existing background if present
                }
            } // end FirebaseScoreUpdater

            // Create instance (will attach listeners to live_data_dumps and per-match matchstats)
            const firebaseUpdater = new FirebaseScoreUpdater();

            // Expose a minimal test helper (no console)
            window.testFirebase = async function() {
                try {
                    // fetch all matchIds once (read-only)
                    const dumpsRef = ref(db, 'live_data_dumps');
                    const snap = await get(dumpsRef);
                    // no console output - callers can inspect snap if they want
                    return snap.exists() ? Object.keys(snap.val() || {}) : [];
                } catch (e) {
                    return [];
                }
            };
        </script>

<script>
// Format match dates in user's local timezone
document.querySelectorAll('.match-date-time[data-utc]').forEach(function (el) {
    var d = new Date(el.getAttribute('data-utc'));
    if (!isNaN(d.getTime())) {
        var days = ['Sun', 'Mon', 'Tue', 'Wed', 'Thu', 'Fri', 'Sat'];
        var months = ['Jan', 'Feb', 'Mar', 'Apr', 'May', 'Jun', 'Jul', 'Aug', 'Sep', 'Oct', 'Nov', 'Dec'];
        var dd = String(d.getDate()).padStart(2, '0');
        var hh = String(d.getHours()).padStart(2, '0');
        var mm = String(d.getMinutes()).padStart(2, '0');
        var formatted = days[d.getDay()] + ' ' + months[d.getMonth()] + ' ' + dd + ' | ' + hh + ':' + mm;
        el.textContent = formatted.toUpperCase();
    }
});

// Auto-scroll script - runs after everything else loads
window.addEventListener('load', function() {
    // Function to auto-scroll to live matches
    function autoScrollToLiveMatch() {
        const activeContainer = document.querySelector('.round-fixtures[style*="block"]');
        if (!activeContainer) return;

        const track = activeContainer.querySelector('.fixtures-track');
        if (!track) return;

        const liveMatches = activeContainer.querySelectorAll('.fixture-card.fixture-live');

        if (liveMatches.length > 0) {
            const firstLiveMatch = liveMatches[0];

            // Simple scroll to center
            const trackWidth = track.clientWidth;
            const matchWidth = firstLiveMatch.offsetWidth;
            const matchLeft = firstLiveMatch.offsetLeft;
            const scrollPosition = matchLeft - (trackWidth / 2) + (matchWidth / 2);

            track.scrollTo({
                left: Math.max(0, scrollPosition),
                behavior: 'smooth'
            });
        }
    }

    // Auto-scroll on page load
    setTimeout(function() {
        autoScrollToLiveMatch();
    }, 1000);

    // Auto-scroll when switching rounds
    const roundItems = document.querySelectorAll('.round-item');

    for (let i = 0; i < roundItems.length; i++) {
        roundItems[i].addEventListener('click', function() {
            setTimeout(function() {
                autoScrollToLiveMatch();
            }, 500);
        });
    }

    // Expose function for testing
    window.testAutoScroll = autoScrollToLiveMatch;
});

// --- TITLE UPDATE (no page reload; supports Firebase DOM updates) ---
(function () {
    var FULLTIME_HIDE_DELAY_MS = 2000;

    function sanitizeCarouselCards() {
        var track = document.querySelector('.fixtures-slider .fixtures-track');
        if (!track) return;

        var cards = Array.prototype.slice.call(track.querySelectorAll('.fixture-card'));
        if (!cards.length) return;

        var visibleCards = [];

        // Non-destructive filtering so cards can reappear if Firebase updates them later.
        // Completed cards are kept visible briefly (2s) after LIVE->FT transition, then hidden.
        cards.forEach(function (card) {
            card.classList.remove('fixture-hidden-completed', 'fixture-hidden-overflow');

            var isCompleted = card.classList.contains('fixture-post') || !!card.querySelector('.fulltime-indicator');
            if (isCompleted) {
                var fulltimeAtRaw = card.getAttribute('data-fulltime-at');
                var fulltimeAt = fulltimeAtRaw ? Number(fulltimeAtRaw) : NaN;
                var hasGraceTimer = Number.isFinite(fulltimeAt);
                var withinGrace = hasGraceTimer && (Date.now() - fulltimeAt < FULLTIME_HIDE_DELAY_MS);

                if (withinGrace) {
                    visibleCards.push(card);
                } else {
                    card.classList.add('fixture-hidden-completed');
                }
                return;
            }

            card.removeAttribute('data-fulltime-at');
            visibleCards.push(card);
        });

        // Max 8 visible (reserve pool beyond 8 stays overflow-hidden).
        visibleCards.slice(8).forEach(function (card) {
            card.classList.add('fixture-hidden-overflow');
        });
    }

    function updateFixturesTitleFromDom() {
        var titleEl = document.querySelector('.fixtures-slider .fixtures-title');
        if (!titleEl) return;

        // "LIVE" state is driven by matchStatus = "in-play" (mapped in markup to .fixture-live)
        var hasLiveCard = !!document.querySelector('.fixtures-slider .fixture-card.fixture-live:not(.fixture-hidden-completed):not(.fixture-hidden-overflow)');
        var desired = hasLiveCard ? 'LIVE & UPCOMING MATCHES' : 'UPCOMING MATCHES';
        if (titleEl.textContent.trim() !== desired) {
            titleEl.textContent = desired;
        }
    }

    if (document.readyState === 'loading') {
        document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function () {
            sanitizeCarouselCards();
            updateFixturesTitleFromDom();
        });
    } else {
        sanitizeCarouselCards();
        updateFixturesTitleFromDom();
    }

    var slider = document.querySelector('.fixtures-slider');
    if (slider && 'MutationObserver' in window) {
        var observer = new MutationObserver(function () {
            sanitizeCarouselCards();
            updateFixturesTitleFromDom();
        });
        observer.observe(slider, { childList: true, subtree: true });
    }

    setInterval(function () {
        sanitizeCarouselCards();
        updateFixturesTitleFromDom();
    }, 500);
})();
</script>

<style>
.fixtures-slider .fixture-card.fixture-hidden-completed,
.fixtures-slider .fixture-card.fixture-hidden-overflow {
    display: none !important;
}
</style>

        <script type="module">
            // Import Firebase SDK (modular)
            import { initializeApp } from "https://www.gstatic.com/firebasejs/12.2.1/firebase-app.js";
            import { getDatabase, ref, onChildAdded, onChildChanged, onValue, get } from "https://www.gstatic.com/firebasejs/12.2.1/firebase-database.js";

            // Firebase configuration (from PHP env)
            const firebaseConfig = {
                apiKey: "AIzaSyDctv2PzSMiEMnk66rC8ZoxGHFygbj3LQI",
                authDomain: "urc-data-push-notifier.firebaseapp.com",
                databaseURL: "https://urc-data-push-notifier-default-rtdb.europe-west1.firebasedatabase.app",
                projectId: "urc-data-push-notifier",
                storageBucket: "urc-data-push-notifier.firebasestorage.app",
                messagingSenderId: "739085063936",
                appId: "1:739085063936:web:29a3f648bbf2130a83c5e5",
                measurementId: "G-ZSCBKTHN6V"
            };

            // Initialize Firebase
            const app = initializeApp(firebaseConfig);
            const db = getDatabase(app);

            /**
             * FirebaseScoreUpdater
             * - listens for matchId children under live_data_dumps
             * - attaches per-match listener to live_data_dumps/{matchId}/matchstats
             * - updates DOM fixture-cards and home-hero using same functions & names as requested
             * - handles template switching from post-match to live-match
             * - initializes Splide for multiple live matches
             */
            class FirebaseScoreUpdater {
                constructor() {
                    this.updateQueue = new Set();
                    this.updateInProgress = false;
                    this.retryCount = {};
                    this.initialLoad = true;
                    this.initialMatchesProcessed = new Set();
                    this.activeMatchListeners = new Map(); // matchId -> unsubscribe function (if provided)
                    this.currentHeroState = null; // Track current hero state (fixture/in-play/results)
                    this.liveMatchesCache = new Map(); // Cache live match data for hero updates (matchId -> {scoreData, lastUpdated})
                    this.currentHeroMatchId = null; // Track which match ID is currently displayed in home-hero
                    this.matchToShow = null; // Track which match should be shown (from Firebase push)
                    this.debugMode = true; // Debug logging enabled to diagnose live match issues
                    this.templateSwitchInProgress = false; // Prevent multiple simultaneous template switches
                    this.templateSwitchTimeout = null; // No longer used - removed debouncing
