                    this.lastTemplateSwitchTime = 0; // No longer used - removed time-based checks
                    this.splideInitializing = false; // Prevent Splide re-initialization during active init
                    this.checkHeroTemplateSwitchInProgress = false; // Prevent recursive calls to checkHeroTemplateSwitch
                    this.matchesThatTriggeredSwitch = new Set(); // Track matches that have already triggered a template switch (prevent duplicate switches)
                    this.init();
                }

                log(message, data = null) {
                    // Logging disabled to reduce console noise
                    return;
                }

                init() {
                    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
                        const fixtureCards = document.querySelectorAll('.fixture-card[data-match-id]');
                        fixtureCards.forEach(card => {
                            const matchId = card.dataset.matchId;
                            if (matchId) {
                                this.initialMatchesProcessed.add(String(matchId));
                            }
                        });
                        
                        this.detectCurrentHeroState();
                        this.detectCurrentHeroMatch(); // Detect initial match ID
                    });

                    this.setupFirebaseListeners();

                    // Back-forward cache restore: Splide keeps stale transforms/slide widths; refresh layout
                    window.addEventListener('pageshow', (e) => {
                        if (e.persisted) {
                            this.refreshHomeHeroSplideAfterBfCache();
                        }
                    });
                    
                    // Mark initial load as complete immediately - no delay needed
                    // Removed setTimeout to prevent delays
                    this.initialLoad = false;
                    
                    // Periodic check to ensure template state stays correct
                    // Note: This is for ongoing monitoring, not initial page load
                    setInterval(() => {
                        // Clean up completed matches from cache
                        this.cleanupCompletedMatches();
                        // Reconcile fixture cards when a final non-live event is missed.
                        this.reconcileLiveFixtureCards();
                        this.checkHeroTemplateSwitch();
                    }, 2000); // Check every 2 seconds
                }

                /**
                 * Detect current hero state from DOM classes
                 */
                detectCurrentHeroState() {
                    const matchSection = document.querySelector('.home-hero-container .match-section');
                    
                    if (!matchSection) {
                        // this.log('⚠️ No .match-section found in hero');
                        return;
                    }
                    
                    if (matchSection.classList.contains('live-match-bg')) {
                        this.currentHeroState = 'in-play';
                    } else if (matchSection.classList.contains('post-match-bg')) {
                        const hasLiveMatch = document.querySelector('.live-match-container');
                        this.currentHeroState = hasLiveMatch ? 'in-play' : 'fixture';
                    } else {
                        this.currentHeroState = 'fixture';
                    }
                    
                    // this.log(`🎯 Current hero state detected: ${this.currentHeroState}`, {
                    //     hasLiveMatchBg: matchSection.classList.contains('live-match-bg'),
                    //     hasPostMatchBg: matchSection.classList.contains('post-match-bg'),
                    //     hasLiveMatchContainer: !!document.querySelector('.live-match-container')
                    // });
                }

                /**
                 * setupFirebaseListeners
                 * Listen to live_data_dumps for new matchId children and attach per-match listeners
                 */
                // setupFirebaseListeners() {
                //     // this.log('🔌 Setting up Firebase listeners');
                    
                //     try {
                //         const dumpsRef = ref(db, 'live_data_dumps');

                //         // When a new matchId is added under live_data_dumps
                //         onChildAdded(dumpsRef, (childSnap) => {
                //             const matchId = childSnap.key;
                //             if (!matchId) return;
                //             // this.log(`➕ New match child added: ${matchId}`);
                //             // Attach per-match listener for matchstats
                //             this.ensureMatchListener(matchId);
                //         });

                //         // Also handle structured updates to live_data_dumps (initial snapshot + updates)
                //         onValue(dumpsRef, (snapshot) => {
                //             const current = snapshot.exists() ? snapshot.val() : {};
                //             const matchIds = Object.keys(current || {});
                //             // this.log(`📊 Firebase snapshot received, ${matchIds.length} matches`, matchIds);
                //             matchIds.forEach(id => this.ensureMatchListener(id));
                //         });
                //     } catch (err) {
                //         this.log('❌ Error setting up Firebase listeners', err);
                //     }
                // }

                setupFirebaseListeners() {
                    const dumpsRef = ref(db, 'live_data_dumps');

                    // Remove previous listeners if any
                    if (this.mainUnsubscribe) {
                        this.mainUnsubscribe();
                    }

                    // Use only ONE listener to avoid double triggering
                    this.mainUnsubscribe = onValue(dumpsRef, (snap) => {

                        if (!snap.exists()) return;

                        const data = snap.val();
                        const matchIds = Object.keys(data);

                        matchIds.forEach(id => this.ensureMatchListener(id));
                    });
                }

                /**
                 * Attach per-match listener for matchstats if not already attached
                 * Path: live_data_dumps/{matchId}/matchstats
                 */
                ensureMatchListener(matchId) {
                    if (!matchId) return;
                    const id = String(matchId);

                    if (this.activeMatchListeners.has(id)) {
                        return; // already listening
                    }


                    const statsRef = ref(db, `live_data_dumps/${id}/matchstats`);

                    // Use onValue to get the whole matchstats node whenever it changes
                    const unsubscribe = onValue(statsRef, (snapshot) => {
                        const payload = snapshot.exists() ? snapshot.val() : null;
                        // Provide data directly to handler
                        this.handleMatchUpdate(id, 'CHANGED', payload);
                    });

                    // store the unsubscribe (Firebase modular onValue returns an unsubscribe function)
                    this.activeMatchListeners.set(id, unsubscribe);
                }

                /**
                 * handleMatchUpdate(matchId, source, matchData)
                 * If matchData provided, process immediately; otherwise queue for fetching.
                 */
                handleMatchUpdate(matchId, source = 'unknown', matchData = null) {
                    if (!matchId) return;

                    if (matchData) {
                        const scoreData = this.extractScoreData(matchData);
                        
                        // this.log(`🔄 Processing match ${matchId}`, {
                        //     isLive: scoreData.isLive,
                        //     isCompleted: scoreData.isCompleted,
                        //     period: scoreData.period,
                        //     matchStatus: scoreData.matchStatus,
                        //     homeScore: scoreData.home,
                        //     awayScore: scoreData.away
                        // });
                        
                        // Cache live match data with timestamp
                        const wasLive = this.liveMatchesCache.has(matchId);
                        const now = Date.now();
                        
                        if (scoreData.isLive) {
                            // this.log(`🟢 Match ${matchId} is LIVE`);
                            // Store with timestamp to track most recently updated
                            this.liveMatchesCache.set(matchId, {
                                ...scoreData,
                                lastUpdated: now
                            });
                            
                            // If this match just went live, switch to it (but only once per match)
                            if (!wasLive) {
                            const matchIdStr = String(matchId);
                                
                                // Only trigger switch if we haven't already triggered it for this match
                            if (!this.matchesThatTriggeredSwitch.has(matchIdStr)) {
                                    this.matchToShow = matchId; // Mark this match to be shown
                                    this.matchesThatTriggeredSwitch.add(matchIdStr); // Mark that we've triggered switch for this match
                                    // Switch immediately - no debouncing, no delays
                                    // This prevents flicker by switching instantly
                                    this.checkHeroTemplateSwitch();
                                }
                                // If we already triggered a switch for this match, just update the cache
                                // Don't trigger another switch - this prevents multiple reloads
                            } else {
                                // This match was already live - only switch if:
                                // 1. It's a different match AND
                                // 2. We haven't switched recently (debounce) AND
                                // 3. No template switch is in progress AND
                                // 4. We haven't already triggered a switch for this match
                                const currentMatchId = this.detectCurrentHeroMatch();
                                const matchIdStr = String(matchId);
                                
                                if (currentMatchId !== matchId && 
                                    !this.templateSwitchInProgress &&
                                    !this.matchesThatTriggeredSwitch.has(matchIdStr)) {
                                    // this.log(`🔄 Match ${matchId} received Firebase push, will switch from ${currentMatchId} immediately`);
                                    this.matchToShow = matchId; // Mark this match to be shown
                                    this.matchesThatTriggeredSwitch.add(matchIdStr); // Mark that we've triggered switch for this match
                                    // Switch immediately - no debouncing, no delays
                                    this.checkHeroTemplateSwitch();
                                }
                            }
                        } else {
                            if (wasLive) {
                                this.liveMatchesCache.delete(matchId);
                                // Clear the "triggered switch" flag when match ends
                                this.matchesThatTriggeredSwitch.delete(String(matchId));
                                // this.log(`❌ Match ${matchId} completed, removed from live cache`);
                                
                                // Check remaining live matches count
                                const remainingLiveMatches = this.getActuallyLiveMatches();
                                const remainingCount = remainingLiveMatches.length;
                                
                                // If the currently displayed match completed, clear it
                                if (this.currentHeroMatchId === matchId) {
                                    // this.log(`⚠️ Currently displayed match ${matchId} completed, will trigger template switch`);
                                    this.currentHeroMatchId = null;
                                }
                                
                                // If we went from 2+ matches to 1 match, refresh template to remove completed match
                                const homeHeroContainer = document.querySelector('.home-hero-container');
                                if (homeHeroContainer && homeHeroContainer.classList.contains('splide') && remainingCount === 1) {
                                    // Force template refresh to show only the remaining live match
                                    this.switchHeroToLiveTemplate();
                                    return; // Don't call checkHeroTemplateSwitch again
                                }
                                
                                // Check if we need to switch templates
                                this.checkHeroTemplateSwitch();
                            }
                        }
                        
                        this.updateFixtureCards({ [matchId]: scoreData });
                        
                        // REMOVED: Duplicate template switch call that was causing multiple reloads
                        // The template switch is already handled above when match goes live (!wasLive)
                        // No need to call it again here
                        return;
                    }

                    // If no matchData provided, queue for processing
                    this.updateQueue.add(matchId);

                    if (this.updateInProgress) return;

                        this.updateInProgress = true;
                    // Process immediately - no delays
                    this.processUpdates();
                }

                /**
                 * processUpdates()
                 * Fetch matchstats for queued matchIds (if per-match listener did not supply data)
                 */
                async processUpdates() {
                    try {
                        const matchesToUpdate = Array.from(this.updateQueue);
                    this.updateQueue.clear();

                        if (matchesToUpdate.length === 0) {
                        this.updateInProgress = false;
                        return;
                    }

                        const scores = {};

                        for (const matchId of matchesToUpdate) {
                            try {
                                const statsRef = ref(db, `live_data_dumps/${matchId}/matchstats`);
                                const snapshot = await get(statsRef);
                                if (snapshot.exists()) {
                                    const matchData = snapshot.val();
                                    const scoreData = this.extractScoreData(matchData);
                                    scores[matchId] = scoreData;
                                    
                                    // Cache live match data with timestamp
                                    const now = Date.now();
                                    const wasLive = this.liveMatchesCache.has(matchId);
                                    
                                    if (scoreData.isLive) {
                                        this.liveMatchesCache.set(matchId, {
                                            ...scoreData,
                                            lastUpdated: now
                                        });
                                        
                                        // If match just went live and we haven't triggered switch yet
                                        if (!wasLive) {
                                            const matchIdStr = String(matchId);
                                    if (!this.matchesThatTriggeredSwitch.has(matchIdStr)) {
                                                this.matchToShow = matchId;
                                        this.matchesThatTriggeredSwitch.add(matchIdStr);
                                                // Trigger template switch immediately - no debouncing
                                                this.checkHeroTemplateSwitch();
                                            }
                                        }
                                    } else {
                                        if (wasLive) {
                                            this.liveMatchesCache.delete(matchId);
                                            // Clear the "triggered switch" flag when match ends
                                            this.matchesThatTriggeredSwitch.delete(String(matchId));
                                            // this.log(`❌ Match ${matchId} completed, removed from live cache`);
                                            
                                            // Check remaining live matches count
                                            const remainingLiveMatches = this.getActuallyLiveMatches();
                                            const remainingCount = remainingLiveMatches.length;
                                            
                                            // If the currently displayed match completed, clear it
                                            if (this.currentHeroMatchId === matchId) {
                                                // this.log(`⚠️ Currently displayed match ${matchId} completed, will trigger template switch`);
                                                this.currentHeroMatchId = null;
                                            }
                                            
                                            // If we went from 2+ matches to 1 match, refresh template to remove completed match
                                            const homeHeroContainer = document.querySelector('.home-hero-container');
                                            if (homeHeroContainer && homeHeroContainer.classList.contains('splide') && remainingCount === 1) {
                                                // Force template refresh to show only the remaining live match
                                                this.switchHeroToLiveTemplate();
                                                return; // Don't call checkHeroTemplateSwitch again
                                            }
                                            
                                            // Check if we need to switch templates
                                            this.checkHeroTemplateSwitch();
                                        }
                                    }
                                } else {
                                    // skip silently if no data
                            }
                        } catch (err) {
                                // increment retry count (no console)
                                this.retryCount[matchId] = (this.retryCount[matchId] || 0) + 1;
                        }
                    }

                        this.updateFixtureCards(scores);
                        
                        // Check if hero template switch is needed
                    this.checkHeroTemplateSwitch();

                    } catch (err) {
                        // silent error handling
                    } finally {
                        this.updateInProgress = false;
                    if (this.updateQueue.size > 0) {
                            // Process immediately - no delays
                            this.processUpdates();
                        }
                    }
                }

                /**
                 * extractScoreData(matchData)
                 * Normalizes many possible shapes into a { home, away, isLive, ... } object
                 */
                extractScoreData(matchData) {
                    let home = null, away = null, period = 'pre', matchStatus = null;

                    if (!matchData || typeof matchData !== 'object') {
                        return {
                            home: null,
                            away: null,
                            isLive: false,
                            period: 'pre',
                            matchStatus: null,
                            hasScoreData: false,
                            isCompleted: false
                        };
                    }

                    if (matchData.matchPeriod || matchData.last_updated || matchData.homeTeam || matchData.awayTeam) {
                        const rawPeriod = matchData.matchPeriod || matchData.period || 'pre';
                        // Normalize "pre match" to "pre" since they mean the same thing
                        // NOTE: This normalization ONLY affects "pre match" -> "pre". Other periods like "post", "in-play", "live" remain unchanged.
                        period = (String(rawPeriod).toLowerCase() === 'pre match' || String(rawPeriod).toLowerCase() === 'pre-match') ? 'pre' : rawPeriod;
                        matchStatus = matchData.matchStatus || null;
                        home = matchData.homeTeam?.score?.currentScore ?? matchData.homeTeam?.score ?? matchData.homeScore ?? matchData.home_score ?? matchData.home ?? null;
                        away = matchData.awayTeam?.score?.currentScore ?? matchData.awayTeam?.score ?? matchData.awayScore ?? matchData.away_score ?? matchData.away ?? null;
                    } else {
                        home = matchData.homeScore ?? matchData.home_score ?? matchData.home ?? null;
                        away = matchData.awayScore ?? matchData.away_score ?? matchData.away ?? null;
                        const rawPeriod = matchData.period || 'pre';
                        // Normalize "pre match" to "pre" since they mean the same thing
                        // NOTE: This normalization ONLY affects "pre match" -> "pre". Other periods like "post", "in-play", "live" remain unchanged.
                        period = (String(rawPeriod).toLowerCase() === 'pre match' || String(rawPeriod).toLowerCase() === 'pre-match') ? 'pre' : rawPeriod;
                        matchStatus = matchData.matchStatus || null;
                    }

                    const parseOrNull = (v) => (v !== null && v !== undefined && v !== '') ? parseInt(v, 10) : null;

                    const hasScores = (home !== null && home !== undefined) || (away !== null && away !== undefined);
                    const homeNum = parseOrNull(home);
                    const awayNum = parseOrNull(away);

                    const periodStr = String(period ?? '').toLowerCase().trim();
                    const periodCompact = periodStr.replace(/\s+/g, '');
                    const statusStr = String(matchStatus ?? '').toLowerCase().trim();
                    const statusCompact = statusStr.replace(/\s+/g, '');

                    // Check if match is completed FIRST - this takes priority
                    // Note: "pre match" should NEVER be treated as completed, regardless of scores
                    // APIs often send "Full Time", "full time", or "fulltime" — treat all as finished.
                    const isCompleted = (
                        periodStr === 'post' ||
                        periodStr === 'result' ||
                        periodStr === 'full time' ||
                        periodStr === 'full-time' ||
                        periodCompact === 'fulltime' ||
                        periodStr === 'ft' ||
                        statusStr === 'result' ||
                        statusStr === 'completed' ||
                        statusStr === 'full-time' ||
                        statusStr === 'full time' ||
                        statusCompact === 'fulltime' ||
                        statusStr === 'ft' ||
                        statusStr === 'ended'
                    );

                    // A match is NOT live if it's completed, regardless of other indicators
                    // IMPORTANT: "in-play" matchStatus means the match is live (checked below)
                    // IMPORTANT: "post" period means the match is over (checked in isCompleted above)
                    const livePeriods = ['live', 'first half', 'second half', 'extra time', 'in-play'];
                    const isLive = !isCompleted && (
                        livePeriods.includes(periodStr) ||
                        statusStr === 'in-play' ||
                        statusStr === 'live'
                    );

                    return {
                        home: homeNum,
                        away: awayNum,
                        isLive: !!isLive,
                        period: period,
                        matchStatus: matchStatus,
                        hasScoreData: !!hasScores,
                        isCompleted: !!isCompleted
                    };
                }

                /**
                 * updateFixtureCards(scores)
                 * scores: { matchId: scoreData, ... }
                 */
                updateFixtureCards(scores) {
                    if (!scores || Object.keys(scores).length === 0) return;

                    let hasUpdates = false;

                    Object.entries(scores).forEach(([matchId, scoreData]) => {
                        const fixtureCard = document.querySelector(`.fixture-card[data-match-id="${matchId}"]`);
                        if (fixtureCard) {
                            fixtureCard.setAttribute('data-firebase-seen-at', String(Date.now()));
                            const hasTeamScores = fixtureCard.querySelector('.team-scores');
                            const hasTeamInfo = fixtureCard.querySelector('.team-info');

                            // On initial load, don't modify cards that already have correct COMPLETED state from PHP
                            // But ALWAYS allow live matches to update (they need real-time score updates)
                            if (this.initialLoad && hasTeamScores && !scoreData.isLive) {
                                // Check if the card is already in the correct completed state
                                const isCardInPostState = fixtureCard.classList.contains('fixture-post');
                                if (isCardInPostState && scoreData.isCompleted) {
                                    // Card already correctly rendered as completed by PHP, skip modification
                                    return;
                                }
                            }

                            // Don't change to pre if card already shows scores
                            // BUT: Always process completed matches to ensure scores are shown
                            const cardWasLive = fixtureCard.classList.contains('fixture-live');
                            if (hasTeamScores && !scoreData.isLive && !scoreData.isCompleted && !cardWasLive) {
                                // keep current state
                                return;
                            }

                            const homeScoreEl = fixtureCard.querySelector('.score[data-team="home"]');
                            const awayScoreEl = fixtureCard.querySelector('.score[data-team="away"]');

                            if (homeScoreEl && (scoreData.home !== null && scoreData.home !== undefined)) {
                                const currentHomeScore = (homeScoreEl.textContent || '').trim();
                                const newHomeScore = String(scoreData.home ?? '');
                                
                                // this.log(`📊 Match ${matchId} - Home score check:`, {
                                //     current: currentHomeScore,
                                //     new: newHomeScore,
                                //     willUpdate: currentHomeScore !== newHomeScore && !(newHomeScore === '0' && currentHomeScore !== '0')
                                // });
                                
                                if (currentHomeScore !== newHomeScore && !(newHomeScore === '0' && currentHomeScore !== '0')) {
                                    homeScoreEl.textContent = newHomeScore;
                                hasUpdates = true;
                                    // this.log(`✅ Updated home score for match ${matchId}: ${currentHomeScore} → ${newHomeScore}`);
                                }
                            } else if (scoreData.isCompleted && scoreData.home !== null && scoreData.home !== undefined) {
                                // If match is completed but score element doesn't exist yet, updateLiveStatus will create it
                                hasUpdates = true;
                            }

                            if (awayScoreEl && (scoreData.away !== null && scoreData.away !== undefined)) {
                                const currentAwayScore = (awayScoreEl.textContent || '').trim();
                                const newAwayScore = String(scoreData.away ?? '');
                                
                                // this.log(`📊 Match ${matchId} - Away score check:`, {
                                //     current: currentAwayScore,
                                //     new: newAwayScore,
                                //     willUpdate: currentAwayScore !== newAwayScore && !(newAwayScore === '0' && currentAwayScore !== '0')
                                // });
                                
                                if (currentAwayScore !== newAwayScore && !(newAwayScore === '0' && currentAwayScore !== '0')) {
                                    awayScoreEl.textContent = newAwayScore;
                                hasUpdates = true;
                                    // this.log(`✅ Updated away score for match ${matchId}: ${currentAwayScore} → ${newAwayScore}`);
                                }
                            } else if (scoreData.isCompleted && scoreData.away !== null && scoreData.away !== undefined) {
                                // If match is completed but score element doesn't exist yet, updateLiveStatus will create it
                                hasUpdates = true;
                            }

                            // Update live / status UI
                            this.updateLiveStatus(fixtureCard, scoreData.isLive, scoreData);
                        }

                        // Update home-hero if present
                        this.updateHomeHeroMatch(matchId, scoreData);
                        this.updateHomeHeroState(matchId, scoreData);
                    });

                    if (hasUpdates) {
                        window.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent('firebaseScoreUpdated', { detail: { scores } }));
                    }
                }

                /**
                 * updateLiveStatus(card, isLive, scoreData)
                 */
                updateLiveStatus(card, isLive, scoreData = null) {
                    if (!card) return;
                    const statusContainer = card.querySelector('.match-status-indicator');
                    const hasTeamScores = card.querySelector('.team-scores');
                    const hasTeamInfo = card.querySelector('.team-info');

                    // Don't modify cards that already have the correct completed state from PHP
                    // But ALWAYS allow live matches to update (they need real-time updates)
                    if (this.initialLoad && hasTeamScores && !isLive && scoreData?.isCompleted) {
                        const isCardInPostState = card.classList.contains('fixture-post');
                        if (isCardInPostState) {
                            // Card already rendered as completed by PHP, don't modify
                            return;
                        }
                    }

                    // Allow status-only transitions (e.g. live -> full-time) even if score payload is missing.
                    if (hasTeamScores && !isLive && !scoreData?.hasScoreData && !scoreData?.isCompleted) {
                        return;
                    }

                    if (isLive) {
                        card.classList.add('fixture-live');
                        card.classList.remove('fixture-pre', 'fixture-post');
                        card.removeAttribute('data-fulltime-at');

                        if (!hasTeamScores && hasTeamInfo) {
                            const homeNameEl = card.querySelector('.team-name.home-name');
                            const awayNameEl = card.querySelector('.team-name.away-name');
                            const homeLogoEl = card.querySelector('.team-logo.home-logo');
                            const awayLogoEl = card.querySelector('.team-logo.away-logo');

                            if (homeNameEl && awayNameEl) {
                                const teamScoresHTML = `
                                    <div class="team-scores" style="display:flex !important;">
                                        <div class="team-side">
                                            ${homeLogoEl ? `<img src="${homeLogoEl.src}" alt="${homeNameEl.textContent}" class="team-logo">` : `<div class="team-acronym">${homeNameEl.textContent}</div>`}
                                            <div class="score" data-team="home">${scoreData?.home ?? 0}</div>
                                        </div>
                                        <div class="match-status-indicator">
                                            <div class="live-indicator">LIVE</div>
                                        </div>
                                        <div class="team-side">
                                            <div class="score" data-team="away">${scoreData?.away ?? 0}</div>
                                            ${awayLogoEl ? `<img src="${awayLogoEl.src}" alt="${awayNameEl.textContent}" class="team-logo">` : `<div class="team-acronym">${awayNameEl.textContent}</div>`}
                                        </div>
                                    </div>
                                `;
                                hasTeamInfo.outerHTML = teamScoresHTML;
                                const dt = card.querySelector('.match-date-time');
                                if (dt) dt.style.display = 'none';
                            }
                        } else if (hasTeamScores) {
                            hasTeamScores.style.display = 'block';
                            if (hasTeamInfo) hasTeamInfo.style.display = 'none';
                            const dt = card.querySelector('.match-date-time');
                            if (dt) dt.style.display = 'none';
                        }

                        if (statusContainer) statusContainer.innerHTML = '<div class="live-indicator">LIVE</div>';
                        this.updateMatchActions(card, 'live');
                        // Live/in-play: only FOLLOW LIVE — hide stadium name
                        const venueEl = card.querySelector('.match-venue');
                        if (venueEl) venueEl.style.display = 'none';
                        return;
                    }

                    const cardWasLive = card.classList.contains('fixture-live');
                    card.classList.remove('fixture-live');

                    let isPost = false;
                    if (scoreData) {
                        const hasScores = scoreData.home !== null || scoreData.away !== null;
                        const periodLower = String(scoreData.period || '').toLowerCase().trim();
                        const periodCompact = periodLower.replace(/\s+/g, '');
                        const statusLower = String(scoreData.matchStatus || '').toLowerCase().trim();
                        const statusCompact = statusLower.replace(/\s+/g, '');
                        // IMPORTANT: "pre" or "pre match" should NEVER be treated as completed, regardless of scores
                        const prePeriods = ['pre', 'pre match', 'pre-match'];
                        if (prePeriods.includes(periodLower)) {
                            isPost = false;
                        } else {
                            // Prefer upstream normalized completion when available.
                            // Fallback keeps full-time variants resilient across data providers.
                            const isCompleted = !!scoreData.isCompleted || (
                                periodLower === 'post' ||
                                periodLower === 'result' ||
                                periodLower === 'full time' ||
                                periodLower === 'full-time' ||
                                periodCompact === 'fulltime' ||
                                periodLower === 'ft' ||
                                statusLower === 'result' ||
                                statusLower === 'completed' ||
                                statusLower === 'full time' ||
                                statusLower === 'full-time' ||
                                statusCompact === 'fulltime' ||
                                statusLower === 'ft' ||
                                statusLower === 'ended'
                            );
                            isPost = !!isCompleted;
                            // Fallback: if card was live and feed moved out of live without explicit completed flags,
                            // treat as post so UI can show FT and then hide after grace period.
                            if (!isPost && cardWasLive && !isLive) {
                                isPost = true;
                            }
                        }
                    } else {
                        const hasScores = card.querySelector('.score[data-team="home"]') && card.querySelector('.score[data-team="away"]');
                        const hasLiveIndicator = card.querySelector('.live-indicator');
                        isPost = !!(hasScores && !hasLiveIndicator);
                    }

                        const wasPost = card.classList.contains('fixture-post');
                        card.classList.toggle('fixture-post', isPost);
                        card.classList.toggle('fixture-pre', !isPost);
                        if (!wasPost && isPost) {
                            card.setAttribute('data-fulltime-at', String(Date.now()));
                        } else if (wasPost && !isPost) {
                            card.removeAttribute('data-fulltime-at');
                        }
                        
                    // Ensure team-scores section is visible for completed matches
                    if (isPost) {
                        const hasTeamScores = card.querySelector('.team-scores');
                        const hasTeamInfo = card.querySelector('.team-info');
                        
                        if (!hasTeamScores && hasTeamInfo) {
                            // Extract team names from team-info section
                            const homeNameEl = card.querySelector('.team-name.home-name');
                            const awayNameEl = card.querySelector('.team-name.away-name');
                            const homeLogoEl = card.querySelector('.team-logo.home-logo');
                            const awayLogoEl = card.querySelector('.team-logo.away-logo');
                            
                            if (homeNameEl && awayNameEl) {
                                // Create team scores section with proper structure matching the PHP template
                                const teamScoresHTML = `
                                    <div class="team-scores" style="display: flex !important;">
                                        <div class="team-side">
                                            ${homeLogoEl ? `<img src="${homeLogoEl.src}" alt="${homeNameEl.textContent}" class="team-logo">` : `<div class="team-acronym">${homeNameEl.textContent}</div>`}
                                            <div class="score" data-team="home">${scoreData?.home ?? 0}</div>
                                        </div>
                                        <div class="match-status-indicator">
                                            <div class="fulltime-indicator"><span class="full-text">FULL TIME</span><span class="short-text">FT</span></div>
                                        </div>
                                        <div class="team-side">
                                            <div class="score" data-team="away">${scoreData?.away ?? 0}</div>
                                            ${awayLogoEl ? `<img src="${awayLogoEl.src}" alt="${awayNameEl.textContent}" class="team-logo">` : `<div class="team-acronym">${awayNameEl.textContent}</div>`}
                                        </div>
                                    </div>
                                `;
                                
                                // Replace team-info with team-scores
                                hasTeamInfo.outerHTML = teamScoresHTML;
                                
                                // Hide date-time section
                                const dt = card.querySelector('.match-date-time');
                                if (dt) dt.style.display = 'none';
                            }
                        } else if (hasTeamScores) {
                            // Ensure team-scores section is visible
                            hasTeamScores.style.display = 'block';
                            
                            // Hide team info section if it exists
                            if (hasTeamInfo) {
                                hasTeamInfo.style.display = 'none';
                            }
                            
                            // Hide date-time section for completed matches
                            const dt = card.querySelector('.match-date-time');
                            if (dt) dt.style.display = 'none';
                        }
                    }

                    if (statusContainer) {
                        statusContainer.innerHTML = isPost ? '<div class="fulltime-indicator"><span class="full-text">FULL TIME</span><span class="short-text">FT</span></div>' : '';
                    }

                    this.updateMatchActions(card, isPost ? 'completed' : 'pre');
                    // Stadium name only for pre-match; hide for post (result) and live (in-play)
                    const venueEl = card.querySelector('.match-venue');
                    if (venueEl) venueEl.style.display = isPost ? 'none' : '';
                }

                /**
                 * updateMatchActions(card, state)
                 */
                updateMatchActions(card, state) {
                    if (!card) return;
                    let matchActionsContainer = card.querySelector('.match-actions');
                    const dateTimeEl = card.querySelector('.match-date-time');

                    if (state === 'live') {
                        if (!matchActionsContainer) {
                            matchActionsContainer = document.createElement('div');
                            matchActionsContainer.className = 'match-actions';
                            const insertAfter = card.querySelector('.team-scores') || card.querySelector('.team-info');
                            if (insertAfter && insertAfter.parentNode) {
                                insertAfter.parentNode.insertBefore(matchActionsContainer, insertAfter.nextSibling);
                            } else {
                                card.appendChild(matchActionsContainer);
                            }
                        }
                        matchActionsContainer.innerHTML = `
                            <span class="dot"></span>
                            <span class="action-button follow-live">FOLLOW LIVE</span>
                        `;
                        if (dateTimeEl) dateTimeEl.style.display = 'none';
                    } else if (state === 'completed') {
                        if (!matchActionsContainer) {
                            matchActionsContainer = document.createElement('div');
                            matchActionsContainer.className = 'match-actions';
                            const insertAfter = card.querySelector('.team-scores') || card.querySelector('.team-info');
                            if (insertAfter && insertAfter.parentNode) {
                                insertAfter.parentNode.insertBefore(matchActionsContainer, insertAfter.nextSibling);
                            } else {
                                card.appendChild(matchActionsContainer);
                            }
                        }
                        matchActionsContainer.innerHTML = `
                            <span class="icon-recap"></span>
                            <span class="action-button recap">Recap</span>
                        `;
                        if (dateTimeEl) dateTimeEl.style.display = 'none';
                    } else {
                        if (matchActionsContainer) matchActionsContainer.remove();
                        if (dateTimeEl) dateTimeEl.style.display = '';
                    }
                }

                /**
                 * updateHomeHeroMatch(matchId, scoreData)
                 */
                updateHomeHeroMatch(matchId, scoreData) {
                    const homeHeroContainer = document.querySelector('.home-hero-container');
                    if (!homeHeroContainer) return;

                    const homeHeroMatch = homeHeroContainer.querySelector(`#match-${matchId}`);
                    if (!homeHeroMatch) {
                        if (scoreData.isLive) {
                            this.updateHomeHeroToShowLiveMatch(matchId, scoreData);
                        }
                        return;
                    }

                    const liveBtn = homeHeroMatch.querySelector('.live-btn');
                    if (liveBtn) {
                        if (scoreData.isLive) {
                            liveBtn.textContent = 'LIVE';
                            liveBtn.style.backgroundColor = '#ff4b5a';
                            liveBtn.style.color = '#ffffff';
                        } else if (scoreData.isCompleted) {
                            liveBtn.textContent = 'FULL TIME';
                            liveBtn.style.backgroundColor = '#00bcd4';
                            liveBtn.style.color = '#ffffff';
                        } else {
                            liveBtn.textContent = 'FIXTURE';
                            liveBtn.style.backgroundColor = '#666666';
                            liveBtn.style.color = '#ffffff';
                        }
                    }

                    const liveIndicators = homeHeroMatch.querySelectorAll('.live-indicator');
                    liveIndicators.forEach(indicator => {
                    if (scoreData.isLive) {
                            indicator.textContent = 'LIVE';
                            indicator.style.display = 'block';
                    } else if (scoreData.isCompleted) {
                            indicator.className = 'fulltime-indicator';
                            indicator.innerHTML = '<span class="full-text">FULL TIME</span><span class="short-text">FT</span>';
                            indicator.style.display = 'block';
                        } else {
                            indicator.style.display = 'none';
                        }
                    });
                }

                /**
                 * updateHomeHeroToShowLiveMatch(matchId, scoreData)
                 */
                updateHomeHeroToShowLiveMatch(matchId, scoreData) {
                    const homeHeroContainer = document.querySelector('.home-hero-container');
                    if (!homeHeroContainer) return;

                    const matchSection = homeHeroContainer.querySelector('.match-section');
                    if (matchSection) {
                        matchSection.classList.remove('post-match-bg');
                        matchSection.classList.add('live-match-bg');
                    }

                    const allLiveBtns = homeHeroContainer.querySelectorAll('.live-btn');
                    allLiveBtns.forEach(btn => {
                        btn.textContent = 'LIVE';
                        btn.style.backgroundColor = '#ff4b5a';
                        btn.style.color = '#ffffff';
                    });

                    const allLiveIndicators = homeHeroContainer.querySelectorAll('.live-indicator');
                    allLiveIndicators.forEach(ind => {
                        ind.textContent = 'LIVE';
                        ind.style.display = 'block';
                    });
                }

                /**
                 * updateHomeHeroState(matchId, scoreData)
                 */
                updateHomeHeroState(matchId, scoreData) {
                    const homeHeroContainer = document.querySelector('.home-hero-container');
                    if (!homeHeroContainer) return;
                    const matchSection = homeHeroContainer.querySelector('.match-section');
                    if (!matchSection) return;

                    const homeHeroMatch = homeHeroContainer.querySelector(`#match-${matchId}`);
                    const isCurrent = !!homeHeroMatch;
                    if (!isCurrent) return;

                    if (scoreData.isLive) {
                        matchSection.classList.remove('post-match-bg');
                        matchSection.classList.add('live-match-bg');
                    } else if (scoreData.isCompleted) {
                        matchSection.classList.remove('live-match-bg');
                        matchSection.classList.add('post-match-bg');
                    } else {
                        matchSection.classList.remove('live-match-bg', 'post-match-bg');
                    }

                    if (homeHeroContainer.classList.contains('splide')) {
                        window.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent('heroUpdated', { detail: { matchId, scoreData } }));
                    }
                }

                /**
                 * Switch hero template to live-match view
                 * Fetches live match content via AJAX and injects without page reload
                 */
                async switchHeroToLiveTemplate() {
                    // Prevent multiple simultaneous template switches
                    if (this.templateSwitchInProgress) {
                        // this.log('⚠️ Template switch already in progress, skipping');
                        return;
                    }
                    
                    // No time-based checks - allow immediate switches
                    this.templateSwitchInProgress = true;
                    
                    const homeHeroContainer = document.querySelector('.home-hero-container');
                    if (!homeHeroContainer) {
                        this.templateSwitchInProgress = false;
                        // this.log('❌ No home-hero-container found');
                        return;
                    }
                    
                    const matchSection = homeHeroContainer.querySelector('.match-section');
                    if (!matchSection) {
                        this.templateSwitchInProgress = false;
                        // this.log('❌ No match-section found');
                        return;
                    }
                    
                    try {
                        // Add timestamp to bust cache and ensure fresh Firebase data
                        const url = new URL(window.location.href);
                        url.searchParams.set('_refresh', Date.now());
                        
                        // If we have a specific match to show (from Firebase push), tell server to prioritize it
                        if (this.matchToShow) {
                            url.searchParams.set('_show_match', this.matchToShow);
                        }
                        
                        const response = await fetch(url.toString(), {
                            method: 'GET',
                            headers: {
                                'X-Requested-With': 'XMLHttpRequest',
                                'X-Hero-Refresh': 'true',
                                'Cache-Control': 'no-cache, no-store, must-revalidate'
                            }
                        });
                        
                        if (!response.ok) {
                            // this.log('❌ Fetch failed with status:', response.status);
                            // Clear the flag on error so it can retry
                            if (this.matchToShow) {
                                this.matchesThatTriggeredSwitch.delete(String(this.matchToShow));
                            }
                            return;
                        }

                        const html = await response.text();
                        const parser = new DOMParser();
                        const doc = parser.parseFromString(html, 'text/html');
                        
                        const newMatchSection = doc.querySelector('.home-hero-container .match-section');
                        if (!newMatchSection) {
                            // this.log('❌ No match-section found in fetched HTML');
                            // Clear the flag on error so it can retry
                            if (this.matchToShow) {
                                this.matchesThatTriggeredSwitch.delete(String(this.matchToShow));
                            }
                            return;
                        }
                        
                        const hasLiveMatch = newMatchSection.querySelector('.live-match-container');
                        if (!hasLiveMatch) {
                            // this.log('⚠️ New content does not contain live-match-container');
                            // Clear the flag if content doesn't have live match (might be timing issue)
                            if (this.matchToShow) {
                                this.matchesThatTriggeredSwitch.delete(String(this.matchToShow));
                            }
                            return;
                        }
                        
                        // Destroy existing Splide instance before updating DOM
                        if (homeHeroContainer.classList.contains('splide')) {
                            const existingSplide = homeHeroContainer.splide;
                            if (existingSplide && typeof existingSplide.destroy === 'function') {
                                try {
                                    // Remove is-initialized class before destroying to reset CSS
                                    homeHeroContainer.classList.remove('is-initialized');
                                    existingSplide.destroy();
                                } catch (e) {
                                    // this.log('⚠️ Error destroying Splide', e);
                                }
                                homeHeroContainer.splide = null;
                            }
                            homeHeroContainer.classList.remove('splide');
                            homeHeroContainer.classList.remove('is-initialized');
                        }
                        
                        // Update the match section content immediately - no delays
                        matchSection.innerHTML = newMatchSection.innerHTML;
                        matchSection.className = newMatchSection.className;
                        
                        this.currentHeroState = 'in-play';
                        
                        // Update current hero match ID
                        this.detectCurrentHeroMatch();
                        
                        // Clear matchToShow since we've switched
                        if (this.matchToShow) {
                            const switchedMatchId = String(this.matchToShow);
                            // Clear immediately - no delays needed
                            // Allow immediate re-triggering for new Firebase pushes
                            this.matchesThatTriggeredSwitch.delete(switchedMatchId);
                            this.matchToShow = null;
                        }
                        
                        // Check if we need to initialize Splide based on actual live match count
                        const actuallyLiveMatches = this.getActuallyLiveMatches();
                        const liveMatchCount = actuallyLiveMatches.length;
                        
                        // Count actual live match containers in the new DOM
                        const newLiveMatchContainers = newMatchSection.querySelectorAll('.live-match-container');
                        const newLiveMatchCount = newLiveMatchContainers.length;
                        
                        // Only initialize Splide if we have 2+ live matches in both cache and DOM
                        if (liveMatchCount >= 2 && newLiveMatchCount >= 2) {
                            const newHeroContainer = doc.querySelector('.home-hero-container');
                            const shouldUseSplide = newHeroContainer && newHeroContainer.classList.contains('splide');
                            
                            if (shouldUseSplide) {
                                homeHeroContainer.classList.add('splide');
                                
                                // Ensure Splide structure exists before initializing
                                const track = homeHeroContainer.querySelector('.splide__track');
                                const list = homeHeroContainer.querySelector('.splide__list');
                                const slides = homeHeroContainer.querySelectorAll('.splide__slide');
                                
                                if (track && list && slides.length >= 2) {
                                    // Initialize Splide immediately - no delays, no RAFs
                                    // Only initialize if not already initializing and structure is still valid
                                    if (!this.splideInitializing) {
                                        const stillHasStructure = homeHeroContainer.querySelector('.splide__track') && 
                                                                  homeHeroContainer.querySelectorAll('.splide__slide').length >= 2;
                                        if (stillHasStructure) {
                                            this.initializeSplideForLiveMatches();
                                        } else {
                                            this.splideInitializing = false;
                                        }
                                    }
                                } else {
                                    // Structure not ready - clear flag
                                    this.splideInitializing = false;
                                }
                            } else {
                                // Server didn't return Splide structure, ensure it's removed
                                if (homeHeroContainer.classList.contains('splide')) {
                                    homeHeroContainer.classList.remove('splide');
                                }
                                this.splideInitializing = false;
                            }
                        } else {
                            // Less than 2 live matches - ensure Splide is removed and DOM shows only live match
                            if (homeHeroContainer.classList.contains('splide')) {
                                homeHeroContainer.classList.remove('splide');
                                if (homeHeroContainer.splide && typeof homeHeroContainer.splide.destroy === 'function') {
                                    try {
                                        homeHeroContainer.splide.destroy();
                                    } catch (e) {}
                                    homeHeroContainer.splide = null;
                                }
                                if (homeHeroContainer.splideInstance && typeof homeHeroContainer.splideInstance.destroy === 'function') {
                                    try {
                                        homeHeroContainer.splideInstance.destroy();
                                    } catch (e) {}
                                    homeHeroContainer.splideInstance = null;
                                }
                            }
                            this.splideInitializing = false;
                        }
                            
                            window.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent('heroTemplateSwitched', {
                                detail: { state: 'in-play' }
                            }));
                        
                        // Re-initialize Blaze Stories widgets after template switch
                        this.reinitializeBlazeStories();
                        
                        // this.log('✅ Template switch completed successfully');
                        
                    } catch (error) {
                        // this.log('❌ Error during template switch', error);
                    } finally {
                        // Always clear the flag, even on error
                        this.templateSwitchInProgress = false;
                    }
                }

                /**
                 * Switch hero template back to post-match/fixture view
                 * Fetches updated content via AJAX
                 */
                async switchHeroToPostMatchTemplate() {
                    // this.log('🔄 Starting switch to post-match template...');
                    
                    // Prevent multiple simultaneous template switches
                    if (this.templateSwitchInProgress) {
                        // this.log('⚠️ Template switch already in progress, skipping');
                        return;
                    }
                    
                    // No time-based checks - allow immediate switches
                    this.templateSwitchInProgress = true;

                    const homeHeroContainer = document.querySelector('.home-hero-container');
                    if (!homeHeroContainer) {
                        this.templateSwitchInProgress = false;
                        // this.log('❌ No home-hero-container found');
                        return;
                    }
                    
                    const matchSection = homeHeroContainer.querySelector('.match-section');
                    if (!matchSection) {
                        this.templateSwitchInProgress = false;
                        // this.log('❌ No match-section found');
                        return;
                    }
                    
                    // No delay - proceed immediately
                    // Removed setTimeout to prevent delays
                    
                    try {
                        // this.log('📡 Fetching updated page content...');
                        
                        // Add timestamp to bust cache
                        const url = new URL(window.location.href);
                        url.searchParams.set('_refresh', Date.now());
                        
                        const response = await fetch(url.toString(), {
                            method: 'GET',
                            headers: {
                                'X-Requested-With': 'XMLHttpRequest',
                                'X-Hero-Refresh': 'true',
                                'Cache-Control': 'no-cache, no-store, must-revalidate'
                            }
                        });
                        
                        // this.log('📥 Fetch response received', { status: response.status, ok: response.ok });
                        
                        if (!response.ok) {
                            // this.log('❌ Fetch failed');
                            return;
                        }

                        const html = await response.text();
                        // this.log('📄 HTML received, length:', html.length);
                        
                        const parser = new DOMParser();
                        const doc = parser.parseFromString(html, 'text/html');
                        
                        const newMatchSection = doc.querySelector('.home-hero-container .match-section');
                        if (!newMatchSection) {
                            // this.log('❌ No match-section found in fetched HTML');
                            return;
                        }
                        
                        // this.log('✏️ Updating DOM with new content...');
                            matchSection.innerHTML = newMatchSection.innerHTML;
                            matchSection.className = newMatchSection.className;
                        
                        const hasLiveMatch = newMatchSection.querySelector('.live-match-container');
                        this.currentHeroState = hasLiveMatch ? 'in-play' : 'fixture';
                        
                        // Update current hero match ID
                        this.currentHeroMatchId = null; // Clear it, will be detected on next check
                        this.detectCurrentHeroMatch();
                        
                        // this.log('✅ Match section updated successfully, new state:', this.currentHeroState);
                        
                        if (homeHeroContainer.classList.contains('splide')) {
                            homeHeroContainer.classList.remove('splide');
                            const splideInstance = homeHeroContainer.splide;
                            if (splideInstance && typeof splideInstance.destroy === 'function') {
                                splideInstance.destroy();
                            }
                        }
                            
                            window.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent('heroTemplateSwitched', {
                            detail: { state: this.currentHeroState } 
                        }));
                        
                        // Re-initialize Blaze Stories widgets after template switch
                        this.reinitializeBlazeStories();
                        
                        // this.log('🎉 Template switch to post-match completed');
                        
                    } catch (error) {
                        // this.log('❌ Error during template switch', error);
                    } finally {
                        // Always clear the flag, even on error
                        this.templateSwitchInProgress = false;
                    }
                }

                /**
                 * Clean up completed matches from cache
                 */
                cleanupCompletedMatches() {
                    const toRemove = [];
                    
                    this.liveMatchesCache.forEach((matchData, matchId) => {
                        // Remove if completed or not live
                        if (matchData.isCompleted || !matchData.isLive) {
                            toRemove.push(matchId);
                        }
                    });
                    
                    toRemove.forEach(matchId => {
                        this.liveMatchesCache.delete(matchId);
                        if (this.currentHeroMatchId === matchId) {
                            this.currentHeroMatchId = null;
                        }
                        // this.log(`🧹 Cleaned up match ${matchId} from cache (completed or not live)`);
                    });
                    
                    // if (toRemove.length > 0) {
                    //     this.log(`🧹 Cleaned up ${toRemove.length} completed/stale match(es) from cache`);
                    // }
                }

                /**
                 * Reconcile fixture cards that are still marked live in DOM but are no longer live in cache.
                 * This handles missed final status events during concurrent live matches.
                 */
                reconcileLiveFixtureCards() {
                    const liveCards = document.querySelectorAll('.fixtures-slider .fixture-card.fixture-live[data-match-id]');
                    if (!liveCards || !liveCards.length) {
                        return;
                    }

                    liveCards.forEach((card) => {
                        const matchId = String(card.getAttribute('data-match-id') || '').trim();
                        if (!matchId) return;

                        // Only reconcile cards that have received realtime updates in this session.
                        const seenAtRaw = card.getAttribute('data-firebase-seen-at');
                        const seenAt = seenAtRaw ? Number(seenAtRaw) : NaN;
                        if (!Number.isFinite(seenAt)) return;
                        if (Date.now() - seenAt < 3000) return;
                        // If cache still marks it live, leave it.
                        if (this.liveMatchesCache.has(matchId)) return;

                        const homeEl = card.querySelector('.score[data-team="home"]');
                        const awayEl = card.querySelector('.score[data-team="away"]');
                        const home = homeEl ? parseInt((homeEl.textContent || '').trim(), 10) : null;
                        const away = awayEl ? parseInt((awayEl.textContent || '').trim(), 10) : null;

                        this.updateLiveStatus(card, false, {
                            home: Number.isFinite(home) ? home : null,
                            away: Number.isFinite(away) ? away : null,
                            isLive: false,
                            isCompleted: true,
                            hasScoreData: !!(homeEl || awayEl),
                            period: 'post',
                            matchStatus: 'completed'
                        });
                    });
                }
                
                /**
                 * Get actually live matches by verifying their status from cache
                 * Only returns matches that are truly live (not completed)
                 */
                getActuallyLiveMatches() {
                    const actuallyLive = [];
                    
                    this.liveMatchesCache.forEach((matchData, matchId) => {
                        // Verify the match is actually live (not completed)
                        // matchData can be either direct scoreData or {scoreData, lastUpdated}
                        const isLive = matchData.isLive === true;
                        const isCompleted = matchData.isCompleted === true;
                        
                        if (isLive && !isCompleted) {
                            actuallyLive.push({
                                matchId: matchId,
                                data: matchData
                            });
                        }
                    });
                    
                    return actuallyLive;
                }

                /**
                 * Immediate template switch - no debouncing, no delays
                 * Removed debouncing to prevent flicker and enable instant switches
                 */
                debouncedTemplateSwitch() {
                    // Call checkHeroTemplateSwitch immediately - no debouncing
                    this.checkHeroTemplateSwitch();
                }

                /**
                 * Check if hero template needs to switch from fixture/results to live-match
                 * This verifies actual live status, not just cache presence
                 */
                checkHeroTemplateSwitch() {
                    // Prevent recursive calls - if already checking, skip
                    if (this.checkHeroTemplateSwitchInProgress) {
                        return;
                    }
                    
                    // Don't switch if one is already in progress
                    if (this.templateSwitchInProgress) {
                        return;
                    }
                    
                    const homeHeroContainer = document.querySelector('.home-hero-container');
                    
                    if (!homeHeroContainer) {
                        // this.log('⚠️ No home-hero-container found, skipping template switch check');
                        return;
                    }
                    
                    const matchSection = homeHeroContainer.querySelector('.match-section');
                    if (!matchSection) {
                        // this.log('⚠️ No match-section found, skipping template switch check');
                        return;
                    }
                    
                    // Set flag to prevent recursive calls
                    this.checkHeroTemplateSwitchInProgress = true;
                    
                    try {
                        // Get actually live matches (verify they're not completed)
                        const actuallyLiveMatches = this.getActuallyLiveMatches();
                        const actuallyLiveCount = actuallyLiveMatches.length;
                        const actuallyLiveMatchIds = actuallyLiveMatches.map(m => m.matchId);
                        
                        const hasLiveMatchContainer = !!document.querySelector('.live-match-container');
                        
                        // Detect which match is currently displayed in home-hero
                        const currentDisplayedMatch = this.detectCurrentHeroMatch();
                        const currentMatchIsActuallyLive = currentDisplayedMatch && actuallyLiveMatchIds.includes(String(currentDisplayedMatch));
                        
                        // this.log('🔍 Checking hero template switch', {
                        //     cacheSize: this.liveMatchesCache.size,
                        //     actuallyLiveCount: actuallyLiveCount,
                        //     currentHeroState: this.currentHeroState,
                        //     hasLiveMatchContainer: hasLiveMatchContainer,
                        //     currentDisplayedMatch: currentDisplayedMatch,
                        //     currentMatchIsActuallyLive: currentMatchIsActuallyLive,
                        //     actuallyLiveMatches: actuallyLiveMatchIds
                        // });

                        // SCENARIO 2 first: leave live hero when nothing is actually live.
                        // Must NOT be blocked by splideInitializing (that flag only applies to live/Splide setup).
                        if (actuallyLiveCount === 0 && hasLiveMatchContainer) {
                            this.switchHeroToPostMatchTemplate();
                            return;
                        }

                        if (this.splideInitializing) {
                            return;
                        }
                    
                        // SCENARIO 1: Switch TO live template if we have actually live matches but not showing live view
                        if (actuallyLiveCount > 0 && !hasLiveMatchContainer) {
                            this.switchHeroToLiveTemplate();
                        }
                        // SCENARIO 2 handled above
                        // SCENARIO 3: Currently displayed match is no longer actually live, but other matches are live
                        else if (hasLiveMatchContainer && currentDisplayedMatch && !currentMatchIsActuallyLive && actuallyLiveCount > 0) {
                            this.switchHeroToLiveTemplate();
                        }
                        // SCENARIO 4: Current match not in actually live matches, refresh
                        else if (hasLiveMatchContainer && actuallyLiveCount > 0 && currentDisplayedMatch && !actuallyLiveMatchIds.includes(String(currentDisplayedMatch))) {
                            this.switchHeroToLiveTemplate();
                        }
                        // SCENARIO 5: Multiple actually live matches but Splide not active
                        else if (hasLiveMatchContainer && actuallyLiveCount >= 2 && !homeHeroContainer.classList.contains('splide')) {
                            this.switchHeroToLiveTemplate();
                        }
                        // SCENARIO 6: Only 1 actually live match but Splide is active, remove Splide and refresh
                        else if (hasLiveMatchContainer && actuallyLiveCount === 1 && homeHeroContainer.classList.contains('splide')) {
                            // Always refresh template when going from 2+ to 1 match to ensure completed match is removed
                            // This ensures the DOM is updated to show only the live match
                            this.switchHeroToLiveTemplate();
                        }
                        // SCENARIO 7: Check if a more recently updated match should be shown (Firebase push priority)
                        else if (hasLiveMatchContainer && actuallyLiveCount > 0 && currentDisplayedMatch) {
                            // Find the most recently updated match
                            let mostRecentMatchId = null;
                            let mostRecentTime = 0;
                            
                            actuallyLiveMatchIds.forEach(matchId => {
                                const matchData = this.liveMatchesCache.get(matchId);
                                if (matchData) {
                                    const lastUpdated = matchData.lastUpdated || 0;
                                    
                                    // Prioritize by lastUpdated (most recently updated match)
                                    if (lastUpdated > mostRecentTime) {
                                        mostRecentTime = lastUpdated;
                                        mostRecentMatchId = matchId;
                                    }
                                }
                            });
                            
                            // If we found a most recent match and it's different from current, switch immediately
                            if (mostRecentMatchId && mostRecentMatchId !== String(currentDisplayedMatch)) {
                                // Switch immediately - no time-based checks, no delays
